Anthropic exhibits the clearest positive release profile through Claude Code and product-model fit, OpenAI shows backlash-and-recovery dynamics around GPT-5 and GPT-5.1, Grok-3 is shaped by provider identity and political discourse, and DeepSeek-R1 combines engineering praise with concerns about censorship, access, and reliability. These findings show that model releases are not merely technical updates, but user-facing interventions that reshape sentiment, expectations, and public discussion.

</details>


### [138] [From local kernels to global form: modeling the emergence of musical content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24660)
*Francesco Vitucci,Michele Lorusso,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于重叠滑动窗口的观察驱动马尔可夫核估计机制，用于从单一符号序列而非外生形式划分中推导局部转移核的轨迹。在德彪西《Syrinx》的273个逻辑音符事件上，以A-B-A'结构作为参照进行验证，发现L=6时两个参考边界在音高与时长两个维度均达到Jensen-Shannon最大值，但时长平台的宽度远窄于音高平台。理论分析表明音高值及其宽平台本身不足以作为强证据，而跨维度对齐才与边界敏感性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非齐次马尔可夫音乐模型依赖外生的形式划分（如乐段边界），缺乏从音乐序列内部自动推断局部转移核的观察驱动机制。本文旨在探索一种不依赖先验划分、通过滑动窗口自动估计局部转移核轨迹的方法，并以德彪西《Syrinx》为案例评估其作为自动分段器的潜力。

Method: 采用重叠滑动窗口方法，从单个符号序列中生成局部转移核的轨迹；分别测试绝对音高与记谱时长两个维度的核；以常用的A-B-A'读法作为参照（而非真值），计算Jensen-Shannon散度；并通过500次重合成实验量化与源序列的偏离程度。

Result: L=6时，两个参考边界在音高和时长两个维度均达到Jensen-Shannon最大值；时长平台较窄（64/267比较），音高平台较宽（210/267）。理论最大值1/√(L-1)表明音高数据本身证据力有限，但跨维度对齐与边界敏感性一致。重合成实验暴露了L=2时的精确拷贝简并现象。

Conclusion: 跨维度对齐支持边界敏感性，但两个维度各自的宽平台都排除了将单一曲线视为唯一自动分段器的可能性。该观察驱动机制具有潜力，但需要更精细的方法来提供明确的分段信号。

Abstract: Markov models are established tools for symbolic music, including non-homogeneous formulations. The narrower contribution examined here is an observation-driven estimation mechanism: overlapping sliding windows derive a trajectory of local transition kernels from one symbolic sequence rather than from an exogenous formal partition. We test this mechanism on 273 logical note events from Debussy's Syrinx (1913), using the often-proposed A-B-A' reading as a reference rather than ground truth. We apply the same validation to absolute-pitch and notated-duration kernels. At $L=6$, both reference boundaries attain the Jensen--Shannon maximum in both dimensions; the duration plateau is substantially narrower (64 of 267 comparisons) than the pitch plateau (210 of 267). Because the theoretical maximum for consecutive sliding-window comparisons is set by window geometry and equals $1/\sqrt{L-1}$ for maximal turnover of the entering/leaving transition, the pitch value at $L=6$ and its broad plateau are not, by themselves, strong evidence. Their cross-dimensional alignment is consistent with boundary sensitivity, while the broad plateaus preclude treating either curve alone as a unique automatic segmenter. Five-hundred-draw re-synthesis experiments quantify departure from the source in both dimensions and expose an exact-copy degeneracy at $L=2$.

</details>


### [139] [One Timeline, Many Renderings: A Wolfram Language Paclet for heterogeneous musical output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24683)
*Francesco Vitucci,Michele Lorusso,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为Temporal System的Wolfram Language paclet，它将算法作曲所需的多种输出（Csound乐谱、乐谱符号、OSC实时控制和排练节拍器）统一编译自一个基于有理数节拍时间线的不可变类型化实体存储，通过后端特定契约在不同渲染时间点转换为秒、采样或赫兹，确保所有产出的时间同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的算法作曲管线中，Csound乐谱、乐谱排版、实时控制和排练节拍器等不同产物分别独立编写，导致时间线漂移、难以保持同步，增加了创作和维护的复杂性。

Method: 提出Temporal System，一个Wolfram Language paclet，采用单一不可变的类型化实体存储，基于有理数节拍时间线，通过后端特定契约（backend-specific contracts）编译输出Csound合成、beta MusicXML 4.0、OSC控制以及点击音频产物。时间到秒/采样/赫兹的转换推迟至渲染时进行；Csound音符使用外部.orc文件中的稳定命名乐器，曲线以k-rate信号声明在score p字段上；点击后端从相同拍号和速度派生排练音频并复用Csound序列化器。

Result: 系统从单一存储成功生成四种同步产物（Csound、MusicXML、OSC、click），并描述了其时间层、语义层和渲染契约层的实际权衡，以及专有创作环境在开源生态系统中的局限性。归档补充材料在paclet正式发布前提供了所报告的输出。

Conclusion: 通过统一的时间存储和延迟的单位转换，Temporal System有效解决了多输出算法作曲中的时间不同步问题；但其依赖专有的Wolfram语言环境，在开源生态中的可移植性仍存在限制。

Abstract: One algorithmic composition may require a Csound score, engraved notation, real-time control, and a rehearsal click. Authored separately, their timelines drift. Temporal System is a Wolfram Language paclet that instead compiles one immutable store of typed entities on a rational beat timeline through backend-specific contracts. It emits Csound synthesis, beta MusicXML 4.0, OSC control, and click artifacts that remain synchronized because they share that store. Conversion to seconds, samples, or hertz occurs only at render time. Csound notes use stable named instruments in external .orc files; curves become k-rate signals declared against score p-fields. The click backend derives rehearsal audio from the same meter and tempo and reuses the Csound serializer. We describe the temporal, semantic, and rendering-contract layers, their practical trade-offs, and the limits of this proprietary authoring environment within an otherwise open-source ecosystem. The archived supplement exposes the reported outputs pending paclet release.

</details>


### [140] [The Annotation Bottleneck in Persian Text NLP: Persian as an Annotation-Scarce Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24698)
*MohammadHossein Mortazavi,Mostafa Salehi,Hadi Veisi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视波斯语作为"低资源语言"的标签，提出波斯语应更准确地被描述为"标注稀缺"（annotation-scarce）而非全面资源不足。通过三种量化交叉验证（网络可见度、语音资源回顾、匹配的波斯-英语标注对比），论证标注稀缺是资源生态特征而非语言内在属性，体现在任务/领域覆盖不均、方案不兼容、获取困难等方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语常被笼统标记为低资源语言，但这种分类将不同的稀缺类型混为一谈，无法精确指导资源建设与NLP研究投入。

Method: 综述34个代表性波斯语文本资源（截至2026年7月），并进行三种量化交叉验证：(1) 通过W3Techs和Common Crawl测量波斯语网络可见度；(2) 回顾从FARSDAT到现代语料的语音资源轨迹；(3) 进行匹配的波斯-英语对比，以Common Crawl网络存在度为基线归一化任务特定标注量。

Result: 波斯语约出现在0.9%的已知内容语言网站中，Common Crawl中0.7039%的HTML页面以波斯语为主语言；语音资源从FARSDAT发展到含数百至数千小时语音的语料；标注密度因任务而异：句法和新闻NER较密集，自然语言推理低于网络比例基线。

Conclusion: 证据不支持"波斯语全局标注不足"的简单论断。标注稀缺表现为任务和领域覆盖不均、方案不兼容、获取与文档摩擦，以及专业领域、偏好数据和非标准波斯语变体的监督有限。

Abstract: Persian (Farsi) is often described as a low-resource language in natural language processing, but that label collapses distinct shortages into a single category. This paper argues that Persian is more precisely described as annotation-scarce, provided that the term is understood as a property of its NLP resource ecology rather than an intrinsic property of the language. The review covers 34 representative Persian text resources available by July 2026 and adds three quantitative cross-checks. First, independent web measurements place Persian among roughly the twenty most visible content languages: W3Techs reports Persian on about 0.9% of websites with a known content language, while Common Crawl CC-MAIN-2026-30 identifies Persian as the primary language of 0.7039% of HTML pages. Second, a selective speech review shows a long resource trajectory from FARSDAT to recent corpora containing hundreds or thousands of hours of speech. Third, a matched Persian-English comparison normalizes task-specific annotation volumes by relative Common Crawl web presence. The resulting ratios vary sharply: Persian syntax and news NER are comparatively dense, whereas natural-language inference falls below the web-proportional baseline. The evidence therefore does not support a simple claim that Persian is globally deficient in labeled volume. Instead, annotation scarcity is expressed through uneven task and domain coverage, incompatible schemes, access and documentation friction, and limited supervision for specialist domains, preference data, and varieties beyond standard Iranian Persian.

</details>


### [141] [Lost in Speech: Trilingual Spoken Hallucination Detection Across Audio and Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24707)
*Meruyert Aristombayeva,Jason S. Lucas,Chaewan Chun,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个多语言口语幻觉检测基准（含英语、俄语、哈萨克语共12,013个样本），包含三种类型和三个严重级别的受控幻觉，并补充了290个真实虚假新闻样本。实验表明基于转录文本的检测通常优于直接音频处理，且合成数据训练的检测器在真实假新闻上有较强的迁移能力（宏F1 0.82-0.88）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本幻觉检测已被广泛研究，但口语（语音）幻觉检测仍鲜有探索，尤其缺乏低资源语言的相关研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立首个覆盖多语言（包括低资源语言）的口语幻觉检测基准。

Method: 构建多语言口语幻觉基准：包含英语、俄语、哈萨克语共12,013个新闻样本，通过受控方式生成三种类型的幻觉（三种严重级别），样本包含原文和对应幻觉版本（文本+音频形式）；另补充290个经事实核查的真实虚假新闻样本（俄语225个、哈萨克语65个），通过TTS-ASR流水线进行翻译和渲染。评估方法包括微调的多语言编码器，以及零样本上下文设置下的多模态解码模型，比较基于转录文本与直接音频两种处理路径。

Result: 转录文本检测通常优于直接音频处理；对于强编码器，二元任务性能下降与各语言ASR错误率相关。在真实虚假新闻上，合成训练检测器迁移表现强劲（原始文本宏F1达0.82-0.88）。俄语来源分析揭示了与真实性相关和模型依赖的机器风格信号，量化了合成幻觉基准中的关键混淆因素。

Conclusion: 合成数据训练的口语幻觉检测器在真实场景下具有较好的迁移能力，但研究也揭示了合成基准中存在的模型风格信号这一混淆因素，提示未来构建基准时需注意降低此类偏差。

Abstract: While text-based hallucination detection has been extensively studied, spoken hallucination detection remains largely unexplored, particularly for low-resource languages. We present the first multilingual spoken hallucination benchmark comprising 12,013 news samples across English, Russian, and Kazakh with controlled hallucinations of three types and three severity levels. Samples comprise original articles and aligned hallucinated counterparts in text and audio. We complement the synthetic corpus with 290 fact-checked fake news items collected natively in Russian (225) and Kazakh (65), translated into the other language and rendered through the same TTS-ASR pipeline. We assess fine-tuned multilingual encoders and, in zero-shot in-context settings, multimodal decoder models on transcript-based versus direct audio processing. Transcript-based detection generally outperforms direct audio processing, with binary-task degradation for strong encoders tracking per-language ASR error. On real-world fakes, synthetic-trained detectors transfer strongly (macro-F1 0.82-0.88 on original text), while Russian provenance analysis reveals both veracity-related and model-dependent machine-style signals, quantifying a key confound in synthetic hallucination benchmarks.

</details>


### [142] [Arbitrary Polygon Oscillator: Generalizing Polygonal Synthesis to Arbitrary Shapes, Morphing, and Three-Dimensional Polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24726)
*Antonio Argentieri,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的弧长引擎多边形合成系统，支持任意顶点配置的多边形（规则、不规则或星形），通过混合插值实现不同顶点数多边形间的平滑变形，并扩展至三维凸多面体旋转切割生成动态截面多边形。系统在RNBO中实现，采用几何缓存策略和四点polyBLAMP抗混叠技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多边形合成局限于常角速度的规则参数化多边形，只能在同一形状族内产生平滑音色变化。本文旨在突破这一限制，构建一个通用的弧长引擎，使任意来源的顶点数据都能驱动同一数字信号处理流水线，拓宽波形生成的形状空间。

Method: （1）改进振荡器以接受外部缓冲区中的任意顶点配置，支持规则、不规则及星形多边形； （2）提出混合插值算法，实现顶点数不同的多边形之间的平滑变形，生成无参数描述的中间形状； （3）将范式扩展至三维：凸多面体绕三轴旋转后被固定水平面切割，截面产生连续变化的多边形； （4）系统在RNBO中实现，采用几何缓存策略避免逐样本重算； （5）抗混叠结合四点polyBLAMP校正（基于运行时贝塞尔切线）与自适应过采样。

Result: 系统成功实现了对任意顶点配置多边形的统一波形生成，支持不同顶点数多边形间的平滑混合，以及三维旋转驱动的动态截面合成。抗混叠方案无需针对每个形状进行解析推导即可适应一般顶点配置。

Conclusion: 本文建立了通用的弧长多边形合成框架，将波形设计空间从规则多边形族扩展至任意多边形及三维衍生截面，为电子音乐和声音设计提供了更丰富的音色探索工具。

Abstract: Polygonal synthesis generates audio by traversing the perimeter of a polygon with a phasor; prior work uses a constant angular velocity, whereas the proposed system adopts constant arc-length (perimeter) velocity. Existing formulations operate on regular, parametrically defined polygons, producing smooth timbral transitions within a single family of shapes.
  This paper generalizes polygonal synthesis around a unified arc-length engine: vertex data of any origin feed the same DSP pipeline. First, we adapt the oscillator to accept arbitrary vertex configurations from an external buffer, opening the possibility for a broad class of closed polygons -- regular, irregular, or star-shaped -- to function as a waveform generator. Second, a hybrid interpolation algorithm enables smooth morphing between polygons with unequal vertex counts, passing through intermediate shapes that have no parametric description. Third, we extend the paradigm to three dimensions: a convex polyhedron rotated about three axes is sliced by a fixed horizontal plane, and the resulting cross-section yields a continuously variable polygon controlled by the solid's orientation. The system runs in RNBO (Cycling~'74) with a geometry caching strategy that avoids per-sample recomputation. Antialiasing combines a four-point polyBLAMP correction derived from runtime Bézier tangents with adaptive oversampling, adapting the correction geometrically to general vertex configurations without per-shape analytical derivation.

</details>


### [143] [SkillForge: Evolving Verifiable Skills for Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24747)
*Shidong Yang,Ziyu Ma,Tongwen Huang,Xucong Wang,Renda Li,Yiming Hu,Yong Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillForge是一个用于LLM智能体持续技能演化的框架，通过环境交互验证和精炼技能库，使技能不仅可积累还能保持有效性，在ALFWorld、WebShop和AppWorld上优于SkillRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RL训练的LLM智能体大多是回合制的，无法跨回合积累可复用知识。已有技能学习方法（如SkillRL）将技能库视为只追加的仓库，不验证已存技能是否仍然有效，导致技能质量随时间退化。

Method: SkillForge通过三个关键设计实现持续技能演化：(1) 在智能体交互过程中显式使用技能，使RL能同时优化环境动作和技能调用决策；(2) 引入基于证据的技能验证机制；(3) 提出多路径技能归纳方法，使技能库能够持续增长同时维持质量。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和AppWorld三个基准上，SkillForge持续优于SkillRL，验证了持续验证的技能在训练更强的LLM智能体方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过让技能在使用中被验证和精炼，而不是仅仅不断追加，SkillForge能够持续保持技能库质量，从而训练出更强大的LLM智能体。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are trained with reinforcement learning (RL) for complex decision-making tasks. However, most RL-trained agents remain episodic and cannot accumulate reusable knowledge across episodes. Recent skill-based approaches, such as SkillRL, attempt to address this issue by extracting skills from raw trajectories, but treat the skill bank as an append-only repository without verifying whether stored skills remain effective. In this paper, we propose SkillForge, a framework for continuous skill evolution that enables skills to be verified and refined through environment interaction. By making skill usage explicit during agent interaction, RL can directly optimize both environment actions and skill invocation decisions. SkillForge further introduces evidence-based skill verification and multi-pathway skill induction, allowing the skill bank to continuously grow while maintaining its quality. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and AppWorld show that SkillForge consistently outperforms SkillRL, demonstrating the effectiveness of continuously verified skills in training stronger LLM agents.

</details>


### [144] [The RAT: A Unified Bayesian Model for RAG Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24753)
*Pius von Däniken,Felix Matthias Saaro,Mark Cieliebak,Jan Deriu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯评估框架，用于联合建模RAG系统中的检索成功、弃答行为和答案正确性，通过条件分解揭示不同系统组件间的行为差异，并支持将人类标注与LLM-as-a-judge标注统一整合到概率模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的RAG评估仅关注端到端的正确性，忽略了各组件之间的交互关系以及错误在流水线中的传播方式，导致表面上指标等效的系统可能存在实质性的行为差异。

Method: 构建了一个贝叶斯评估框架，根据流水线的信息流对检索成功、弃答行为和答案正确性进行联合建模和条件分解，并扩展到将LLM-as-a-judge标注作为校准后的噪声观测纳入统一概率模型；同时在27种RAG配置（3个数据集×3个检索器×3个生成器）上进行了验证。

Result: 条件分解揭示了在边际指标下看似等效的系统之间存在显著的行为差异；检索成功标注比任务成功标注在估计策略遵从性方面更具信息量；框架成功整合了LLM-as-a-judge标注作为校准噪声观测。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯条件分解框架为RAG系统评估提供了更细粒度的视角，能够帮助实践者更准确地理解系统组件行为并优化标注资源分配。

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems requires assessing not only end-to-end correctness but also how individual components interact and how errors propagate through the pipeline. We introduce a Bayesian evaluation framework that jointly models retrieval success, abstention behavior, and answer correctness, factorized according to the pipeline's information flow. The model distinguishes task success. Whether the user received a correct answer (from generator success) and whether the generator behaved appropriately given the retrieval outcome. We apply the framework to 27 RAG configurations across three datasets, three retrievers, and three generators, and show that the conditional decomposition reveals substantial behavioral differences between systems that appear equivalent under marginal metrics. We further analyze the annotation allocation problem, demonstrating that retrieval-success annotations are more informative than task-success annotations for estimating policy adherence, and provide an information-theoretic explanation for this asymmetry. Finally, we extend the model to incorporate LLM-as-a-judge annotations as calibrated noisy observations, enabling practitioners to combine limited human judgments with cheaper automated assessments within a unified probabilistic model.

</details>


### [145] [ExpConCAD: Experience-Guided Text-to-CAD Generation from Shape Descriptions with Implicit Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24760)
*Jingyao Liu,Jinkang Tang,Chen Huang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpConCAD框架，针对文本到CAD中自然语言描述常缺失空间约束的问题，通过先恢复构建结构和约束范围，再检索相关设计经验补齐隐含空间约束，最终生成可执行的CadQuery程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到CAD方法忽视了真实世界描述中常见的空间约束缺失问题，导致生成的CAD程序无法有效构建。

Method: ExpConCAD包含三步：首先恢复预期构建结构和约束作用范围；其次检索针对相似范围的约束补齐经验；最后生成可执行的CadQuery程序。

Result: 大量实验验证了ExpConCAD的有效性，并揭示了构建结构理解和经验记忆在空间约束补齐中的作用。

Conclusion: 缺失的空间约束应基于底层构建结构并结合可复用的设计经验进行推断，ExpConCAD在该任务上取得了有效成果。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD aims to generate executable CAD programs from natural-language descriptions. However, real-world descriptions are often underspecified and omit critical spatial constraints required for valid CAD construction, a challenge that has been largely overlooked by existing methods. In this paper, we argue that missing spatial constraints should be inferred with respect to the underlying construction structure and informed by reusable design experience. Based on this insight, we propose ExpConCAD, an experience-enhanced framework for implicit spatial constraint completion. ExpConCAD first recovers the intended construction structure and constraint scopes, then retrieves relevant constraint-completion experience for similar scopes to complete the missing spatial constraints, and finally generates executable CadQuery programs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of ExpConCAD and provide insights into the role of construction structure understanding and experience memory in spatial constraint completion. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Hotjiashell/ExpConCAD.

</details>


### [146] [Linear Probing Provides Robust and Efficient Detection of Machine-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24780)
*Gerrit Quaremba,Hanqi Yan,Elizabeth Black,Denny Vrandecic,Elena Simperl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了机器生成文本（MGT）与人类书写文本（HWT）在潜空间中的线性可分性与表示质量差异，提出使用简单线性探针作为MGT检测器。实验表明线性探针在4个基准上相较16个基线显著提升跨领域（OOD）检测性能（+11 AUC），且仅需不到100个样本即可接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器生成文本的滥用风险增加，检测MGT变得日益重要。然而现有监督检测器在跨领域（OOD）场景下性能退化严重，且需要大规模多样化的训练数据，因此需要更鲁棒且样本高效的检测方法。

Method: 首先分析MGT与HWT潜表示的低维线性可分性，并从表示质量的系统性差异角度给出合理解释；基于此洞察训练两种简单线性探针变体，在4个基准上对16个基线进行系统评估，并分析探针向量共享的潜在MGT方向及连续"机器度"光谱。

Result: 线性探针在OOD检测上一致提升（+11 AUC），仅需不到100个样本即可达到接近峰值性能。其迁移能力源于探针恢复了在不同设置间泛化的共享潜MGT方向，且探针向量能捕获连续"机器度"光谱，可用于AI编辑文本的细粒度估计。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了MGT与HWT在潜空间中的差异，证明线性探针是鲁棒且样本高效的MGT检测器，为细粒度估计AI编辑文本提供了新思路，并开源了代码。

Abstract: Distinguishing machine-generated text (MGT) from human-written text (HWT) becomes increasingly important due to potential misuse. However, most supervised detectors often degrade out-of-domain (OOD) and require large, diverse training sets. In this work, we analyze the linearity and quality of MGT representations and show that simple linear probes outperform a wide range of detectors while being substantially more sample-efficient. We first show that MGT and HWT latent representations are linearly separable in low-dimensional space, and provide a plausible explanation for this separability through systematic differences in their representation quality. Motivated by these insights, we train two variants of simple linear probes and evaluate them across 4 benchmarks against 16 baselines. Probes consistently improve OOD detection (+11 AUC), requiring solely ${<}100$ samples to reach near-peak performance. We show that this transferability arises because probes recover a shared latent MGT direction that generalizes across diverse settings. Finally, we demonstrate that probing vectors capture a continuous spectrum of ``machineness'', highlighting their potential for fine-grained estimation of AI-edited text. Overall, our work provides insights into latent-space differences between MGT and HWT and demonstrates the potential of linear probes as as robust and sample-efficient MGT detectors. We release our code on~\href{https://github.com/gerritq/mgt_probes}{github}.

</details>


### [147] [Structurally-bounded Agentic Graph Exploration for Evidence-Grounded Scholarly DeepSearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24809)
*Rima Hazra,Sayan Layek,Somnath Banerjee,Soumen Chakrabarti,Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Crase框架，一种有界且可检查的学术搜索替代方案，通过单次搜索、1.5跳引用扩展、基于蕴含关系的剪枝和随机游走排序，在Recall@50上超越深度研究智能体3倍，成本仅为后者的三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度研究智能体采用开放式搜索循环，虽然性能强大但缺乏可解释性，候选集不可控，停止条件不明确，且依赖昂贵的大模型。作者希望构建一个候选集、保留理由和停止条件在推理前都显式固定的可检查方案。

Method: Crase包含四个步骤：(1) 对搜索引擎进行一次查询获取种子论文；(2) 沿1.5跳引用邻域扩展种子论文；(3) 剪除缺乏蕴含支持的引用边；(4) 使用考虑时效性的随机游走对剩余论文排序。

Result: 在LitSearch和另一个基准测试上，使用500K篇论文的arXiv语料库，Crase在Recall@50上比基于专有模型的深度研究智能体最高提升3倍，而成本约为后者的三分之一。

Conclusion: 有界且可检查的搜索策略可以以更低成本达到比开放式深度研究智能体更好的检索性能，暗示在学术搜索场景中，结构化先验设计可能优于无约束的自主探索。

Abstract: We present Crase, a bounded and inspectable alternative to deep research agents for scholarly search. Instead of an open-ended search loop, Crase queries a search engine once for seed papers, expands them along their 1.5-hop citation neighborhood, prunes citation edges whose claims lack entailment support, and ranks the remaining papers with a recency-aware random walk. This makes the candidate set, the reason each paper is kept, and the stopping condition explicit and fixed before inference. On LitSearch and one further benchmarks over a 500K-paper arXiv corpus, Crase outperforms deep research agents built on proprietary models by up to 3$\times$ recall@50 at roughly a third of the cost.

</details>


### [148] [Reading Is Not Using: Retrieval, Judgment, and the Design of AI Financial Research Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24842)
*Miao Liu,Zhizhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型在金融分析中存在"检索-整合鸿沟"：即使能准确检索到风险披露信息，这些信息对投资判断的影响却会随上下文长度增加而降至噪声水平。该模式在多种模型和任务中稳定复现，并通过因果记忆干预证实压缩摘要与原文查找是信息传导的关键途径。不同的工作流架构决定了信息能否有效影响判断——分块摘要流水线会丢失关键信息，而目标化的结构化重述则能恢复信息影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM金融分析系统的评估仅关注信息检索能力，而忽视了检索到的信息是否真正影响模型的判断，存在评估盲区。作者旨在揭示并量化这种"检索-整合鸿沟"。

Method: 设计对照实验：固定焦点公司信息，仅改变无关上下文长度（2,000到128,000 tokens），测量风险披露对投资判断的影响；跨多种模型家族和判断任务复现实验；在真实10-K文件中删除披露进行验证；通过因果记忆干预测试压缩摘要与原文查找的作用；对比分块摘要流水线与目标化结构化重述两种工作流架构。

Result: 风险披露对投资判断的影响随上下文增长跌至噪声水平，但直接检索仍然准确；更强大的模型仅推迟而非消除这一鸿沟；压缩摘要与原文查找共同传导信息；分块摘要流水线会导致相关信息被挤出，而目标化结构化重述可恢复信息影响力。

Conclusion: AI分析师的表现由模型能力与工作流架构共同决定。仅基于检索能力的评估可能认证那些投资判断忽视已检索到信息的系统，需要将"信息是否真正影响判断"纳入评估标准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as AI analysts to process financial disclosures and support AI-assisted investment decisions. Yet such systems are usually evaluated by what they can retrieve, not whether retrieved information affects their judgments. We identify a retrieval-integration gap in long-context financial analysis. Holding focal-firm information fixed and varying only unrelated context from 2,000 to 128,000 tokens, we find that a risk disclosure's influence on investment judgments falls to the experimental noise floor even as direct retrieval remains accurate. The pattern replicates across model families and judgment tasks and in experiments removing real disclosures from actual 10-K filings. More capable models postpone but do not eliminate the gap. Causal memory interventions show that compressed summaries and source-text lookup jointly transmit disclosures into judgments. Workflow architecture determines whether this transmission succeeds: chunk-and-summarize pipelines evict relevant information, whereas a targeted, structured restatement adjacent to the decision restores its influence. AI analyst performance is therefore jointly determined by model capability and workflow architecture. Retrieval-based evaluations can certify systems whose investment judgments ignore information they demonstrably retrieved.

</details>


### [149] [BrowserForge: Scaling Web Episode via Parallel Browser Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24848)
*Fei Tang,Huawen Shen,Zhiqiong Lu,Zhengxi Lu,Pengyuan Lyu,Chengquan Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BrowserForge是一个在开放互联网上大规模并行驱动浏览器沙箱来生成网页交互数据的框架，通过多来源网页收集、并行沙箱调度和Proposer-Solver双智能体循环，生成了包含203,238条轨迹的大规模数据集，每条轨迹来自不同网站。在生成的语料上微调多模态模型后，在Online-Mind2Web上的成功率从25.66%提升到33.33%，在Multimodal-Mind2Web上的步骤准确率也持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于渲染像素操作的网页智能体虽避免了HTML解析带来的脆弱性和高token成本，但训练这类智能体需要大量高质量交互轨迹，而现有公共数据集通常只有几千条轨迹且局限于固定网站集合。即使近年来的自动合成流水线也受限于预定义网站列表，导致智能体见过的网站多样性严重不足，缺乏在开放网络上规模化生成交互数据的方法。

Method: BrowserForge框架由三个组件构成：1) 开放网络网页收集阶段，暴露智能体于数十万个真实可访问网站；2) 沙箱集群管理器，调度数百个并发浏览器确保高利用率；3) Proposer-Solver双智能体循环，将原始网页转化为可执行任务并收集经过验证的轨迹。此外，规则加模型的清洗流水线会剔除失败运行，并将保留的推理过程重写为统一链式思维风格。可访问性树等页面结构仅作为合成时的信号使用，训练和发布的智能体纯依赖截图输入。

Result: 生成的语料包含203,238条轨迹，每条来自不同网站，在规模和多样性上超过以往所有轨迹数据集。在实时Online-Mind2Web上，微调紧凑型多模态模型后成功率从25.66%提升至33.33%；在静态Multimodal-Mind2Web上步骤准确率一致提升，且随语料规模增大增益增加。受控分析进一步确认开放网络来源和广泛网站覆盖是提升效果的关键因素。

Conclusion: 开放网络数据生成是突破网页智能体训练数据瓶颈的有效途径。BrowserForge通过大规模并行浏览器沙箱和双智能体任务生成机制，成功构建了规模与多样性远超以往的数据集，验证了数据规模与多样性对像素级网页智能体性能的显著推动作用，为后续研究提供了可扩展的数据生成范式。

Abstract: Web agents that act from rendered pixels avoid the fragility and heavy token cost of reading a page's HTML or accessibility tree, but training them depends on large amounts of high-quality interaction trajectories, and how to produce such data at scale remains an open problem. Public datasets typically contain only a few thousand trajectories drawn from a fixed and narrow set of websites, and even recent automated synthesis pipelines stay bound to predefined site lists or tutorial sources, so the number of distinct websites the agent ever sees barely grows. We present BrowserForge, a framework that generates web interaction data at scale by driving many browser sandboxes in parallel over the open web. BrowserForge couples three components: an open-web sourcing stage that exposes the agent to hundreds of thousands of real, openly reachable websites; a sandbox cluster manager that schedules hundreds of concurrent browsers with high utilization; and a Proposer-Solver dual-agent loop that turns a raw page into an executable task and then collects a verified trajectory for it. A rule-plus-model cleaning pipeline removes failed runs and rewrites the surviving reasoning into a single unified chain-of-thought style. Page structure such as the accessibility tree is used only as a synthesis-time signal; the agent we train and release acts purely from the screenshot. The resulting corpus contains 203,238 trajectories, each collected from a distinct website, larger and more diverse than prior trajectory datasets. Fine-tuning a compact multimodal model on this corpus raises its success rate on the live Online-Mind2Web from 25.66% to 33.33% and consistently improves step accuracy on the static Multimodal-Mind2Web, with the gain growing as the corpus scales. Controlled analyses further confirm that open-web sourcing and broad website coverage are key contributors to the observed improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Decomposing Browser Pipeline Architectures for DOM-Sourced Particle Effects: Worker Offload, WebGL, and WebAssembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23609)
*Hossein Asadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过五个粒子管线（P1-P5）加一个CSS基线（P0）的可控架构分解实验，系统隔离了线程放置、渲染器选择和模拟后端这三个层次，揭示了浏览器分层架构中"局部优化不等于端到端优化"的核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web Workers、WebGL和WebAssembly常被视为可互换的"加速手段"，但每种技术作用于不同架构层次。作者通过实证研究揭示局部层优化（如WASM加速模拟）未必能转化为用户可见的整体帧率提升。

Method: 以DOM源粒子管线为工作负载，构建5个粒子管线（P1-P5）及1个CSS非粒子基线（P0），在可复现测试框架下测量交互帧率、高负载端到端压力及纯模拟微基准；在双浏览器（Firefox 153+Intel UHD、Chrome 138+NVIDIA NVK）及Colab T4（Chrome 150，n=5）上进行跨主机验证。

Result: （1）Worker卸载提升Chrome交互帧率（约144 vs 52 FPS，Cliff's delta=1）；（2）AssemblyScript加速Chrome更新内核约1.5-1.6x（主主机）和1.85x（Colab T4）；（3）25万WebGL粒子下WASM模拟优势未转化为产品级FPS提升，且CPU阶段计时显示模拟仍占主导预算；（4）渲染器排名和绝对余量随浏览器/GPU/主机配置而变化。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是提出瓶颈感知的架构测量方法：先识别主导性能层，再验证该层优化是否传播到用户可见帧率，避免"局部胜利、全局失效"的优化陷阱。

Abstract: Local optimization does not necessarily yield end-to-end optimization in layered browser architectures. Teams often treat Web Workers, WebGL, and WebAssembly as interchangeable ways to "make it faster," yet each lever targets a different layer. We present a controlled architectural decomposition--using DOM-sourced particle pipelines as a concrete workload--of five particle pipelines (P1-P5), with an additional non-particle CSS-layer baseline (P0), that isolates thread placement, renderer choice, and simulation backend. Using a reproducible harness we measure interactive pacing, high-load end-to-end stress, and a simulation-only microbenchmark, plus same-host cross-browser / dual-GPU-class replication (Firefox 153+Intel UHD; Chrome 138+NVIDIA NVK) and an independent second-host slice on Google Colab (Chrome 150, Tesla T4, n=5). Four results stand out. (1) Worker offload improves interactive pacing on paper-primary Chrome (approx. 144 vs approx. 52 FPS; Cliff's delta=1, n=10). (2) AssemblyScript speeds the Chrome update kernel by about 1.5-1.6x on the primary host and approx. 1.85x on Colab T4 (sim-only). (3) Under approx. 250k WebGL particles the Chrome sim-only WASM win need not raise product FPS (P5<=P4 on primary/Firefox; P5 approx. P4 on Colab T4); in-worker CPU phase timers show simulate still dominates the accounted budget, so the non-translation is not a simple "draw dominates CPU" story. (4) Renderer ranking and absolute margins vary across browser/GPU/host configurations. The contribution is bottleneck-aware architectural measurement: identify the dominant layer and test whether a layer win propagates to user-visible FPS.

</details>


### [151] [From Traceability to Justifiability: Accountability Structures in Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23610)
*Rashid Azarang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文从公开材料出发，测量AI系统交付管道的记录能否表达"被评估的对象即被部署的对象"这一声明，以及该声明在声明处是否成立。通过对47个交付平台的双盲文档调查和30个公共仓库的审计工具实证测量，发现记录在结构上普遍缺乏可辩护性（justifiability）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统的评估与部署之间存在信任鸿沟：评估报告声称评估的对象就是部署的对象，但缺乏机制验证这一声称是否成立。作者希望从公开资料出发，系统性地衡量交付平台的记录能否支撑这一关键声明。

Method: 采用两种方法：(1) 对47个交付平台（20个CI/CD、27个模型服务/代理平台）进行两类文档调查，使用固定的三标签协议，进行两次评分（第二次盲评），所有页面按内容哈希和日期固定；(2) 构建一个工具，从管道发布的公开痕迹（exhaust）计算"实现的保障深度"，并与声明的深度进行比较，应用于30个公共仓库的冻结双层框架，进行双盲评分。

Result: (1) 188个双评单元格中，没有任何平台的默认记录能发出行为元组（模型版本、指令、工具定义、检索和运行时配置）的内容寻址身份；盲评中该列默认评分为0/47。不可变的标称版本控制正在成为代理平台的默认答案（16/27），但只是可变指针背后的版本整数。(2) 30个仓库中发现可验证性漏洞：15个采用认证工具（attestation tooling）的仓库中有7个仅发布源码版本，其声明的工作流绑定无法在声明处被检查。可检查的5个采用者中有5个实现了端到端绑定，两个不足都出现在身份绑定环节。

Conclusion: AI交付领域的记录在结构上低于"可辩护性"这一唯一能拒绝转换的阶梯。调查附有到期时钟，并明确说明了哪些证据会推翻每项发现。核心问题在于：记录系统无法表达被评估对象与部署对象之间的内容寻址等价性，即使声明存在，也在身份绑定环节出现可验证性缺口。

Abstract: A pipeline promoting an AI system publishes records claiming the thing evaluated is the thing deployed and that the evidence licensed the transition. We measure, from public material only, whether those records can express that claim and whether it holds where declared. First, a two-class documentation survey of 47 delivery platforms (20 CI/CD, 27 model-serving/agent) under one fixed three-label protocol, graded twice (second pass blind), every consulted page pinned by content hash and date. Across 188 double-graded cells we found no platform whose default record emits a content-addressed identity of the behavioral tuple (model version, instructions, tool definitions, retrieval and runtime configuration); the blind pass grades that column default on zero of 47. Immutable nominal versioning is meanwhile arriving as the agent platforms' default answer (16 of 27): version integers behind mutable pointers, a layer the artifact supply chain already found insufficient. Second, an instrument computes realized assurance depth from a pipeline's published exhaust alone and compares it with the declared depth. Applied to a frozen two-stratum frame of 30 public repositories graded twice from a hashed archive (second pass blind; cell-level agreement 23 and 19 of 30, both passes independently finding the same five full realizations), the sharpest result is a verifiability hole: seven of the 15 repositories chosen for adopting attestation tooling publish source-only releases, so the binding their workflows declare cannot be checked where declared. Where checkable it mostly checks out: five of seven measurable adopters realize the binding end to end; both shortfalls fall at identity binding. Together the results locate the field's records structurally short of justifiability, the one rung that can refuse a transition. The survey carries an expiry clock; we state what would falsify each finding.

</details>


### [152] [REFINE: A Multi-Agent LLM Approach for Evidence-Guided Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23611)
*Muhammad Waseem,Aakash Ahmad,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: REFINE是一个工具无关、基于证据感知的多智能体方法，用于Java文件级代码重构候选生成，在450个Java文件上平均减少约68-73%的代码坏味道，但输出仍需人工审查后才能应用于实际项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型为自动化代码重构提供了新机会，但生成的重构变更必须减少目标质量问题，同时不引入新问题或改变行为相关的代码结构，这一挑战尚未被充分解决。

Method: REFINE采用多智能体流程，包含静态分析引导的坏味道识别、坏味道信息规划、基于LLM的代码转换、自动化再分析、保留性检查和结构化报告六个步骤。

Result: 在450个Java文件（来自15个开源系统）上，使用三种LLM配置（GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview、Claude Opus 4.8）生成1350个输出，分别减少68.26%、72.79%和68.49%的代码坏味道。与直接提示基线相比，REFINE实现了更高的中位数坏味道减少、更小的编辑量和更少的公共方法移除，但整体质量提升不一致，保留性检查发现断言/失败调用变更和公共方法移除等残余风险。

Conclusion: REFINE输出的重构候选需经过编译、测试、依赖分析和人工审查后才能用于仓库级或系统级设置，不能直接自动部署。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new opportunities for automated code refactoring. However, generated changes must reduce targeted quality problems without introducing new issues or altering behaviour-relevant code structures. We introduce REFINE (Refactoring with Evidence-aware Flow for Integrated ageNtic Execution), a tool-agnostic, evidence-aware multi-agent approach for generating Java file-level refactoring candidates. REFINE combines static-analysis-guided smell identification, smell-informed planning, LLM-based transformation, automated re-analysis, preservation checks, and structured reporting.
  We evaluate REFINE on 450 Java files from 15 open-source systems, producing 1,350 model-pass outputs using OpenAI GPT-5.5, Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview, and Anthropic Claude Opus 4.8. REFINE reduces detected code smells by 68.26%, 72.79%, and 68.49% across the three configurations, respectively, with the strongest reductions observed for major smells. A matched 150-file direct-prompt baseline shows that REFINE achieves a higher median code-smell reduction with smaller edits and fewer public-method removals. However, broader quality improvements are inconsistent, and preservation checks reveal residual risks, including assert/fail-call changes and public-method removal. Therefore, REFINE outputs should be treated as refactoring candidates requiring compilation, testing, dependency analysis, and human review before adoption in repository- or system-level settings.

</details>


### [153] [SDR Driver for Precise Timing Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23614)
*Fabrizio Pollastri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文开发了针对低成本HackRF One软件定义无线电的时序驱动，通过AI辅助开发流程将原始固件扩展为SoapySDR兼容驱动，大幅降低了开发工作量并提高了可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本SDR设备HackRF One缺乏精确时序应用所需的驱动支持，需要开发能实现同步采样、时间尺度控制和时钟频率调整的软件驱动。

Method: 首先用Python封装实现初始版本，然后基于AI辅助开发工作流，从现有HackRF One和SoapySDR代码库生成新的SoapySDR兼容驱动，仅需有限的调试即可运行。

Result: AI辅助生成的新驱动仅需有限调试即可投入运行，开发工作量减少了超过一个数量级，同时软件可维护性得到改善。

Conclusion: AI辅助开发方法在SDR驱动开发中能显著降低开发成本并提升代码质量，验证了从现有代码库生成兼容驱动的可行性和高效性。

Abstract: This paper describes the development of software drivers that enable precise timing applications with the low-cost HackRF One software-defined radio (SDR). The original firmware was extended with timing functions for synchronized sampling, time-scale control, and clock-frequency adjustment. An initial implementation based on a Python wrapper was later redesigned as a SoapySDR-compatible driver. Using an AI-assisted development workflow, the new driver was generated from the existing HackRF One and SoapySDR codebases and became operational after only limited debugging. The approach reduced development effort by more than an order of magnitude while improving software maintainability.

</details>


### [154] [Rebuild Dossier: Mechanically-Enforced Specs for Agentic App Rebuilds, and What Model-Tier Failures Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23616)
*Parker Fawcett*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了rebuild-dossier，一个开源工具，通过锁定应用的真实接口（精确输入输出）并以自动化检查强制"一次一测试"的构建流程，来改进AI智能体的代码重建管线。研究发现，规则遵守的智能体可能在保留测试上失败，而规则违反的智能体反而全部通过，说明通过测试套件并不等同于正确性；该工具在小型应用上与基线持平，在大型应用上因检查机制未运行而落后；三层验证（智能体报告、自动化日志、实际产出文件）能捕获单个层级遗漏的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现，一旦模型足够强，多智能体重建管线反而输给最简单的方案（给模型原始代码和一条指令）。作者希望解决这一悖论，即在代码重建前锁定应用的真实接口，并通过自动化检查而非书面指令来约束构建过程。

Method: 提出rebuild-dossier工具：在编写任何代码之前锁定应用的真实接口（精确的输入输出）；通过自动化检查强制执行一次一测试的构建流程；采用三层验证机制——智能体自身报告、自动化日志、实际生成的文件——来交叉核对每一项声明。

Result: 三个主要结果：(1) 小规模比较中，遵守规则的智能体在保留测试上失败而违反规则的智能体全部通过，证明可被博弈的测试套件不能证明正确性；(2) 与"给弱模型源码加一条指令"相比，小应用打平、大应用因自动化检查未运行而彻底落败，指向检查机制而非接口锁定是关键；(3) 三层验证捕获了包括自身日志代码bug在内的真实错误。这些风险在更强的模型和不同工具链上可复现。

Conclusion: 该工具是公开的、MIT许可的开源项目，可端到端复现。研究揭示了测试可被博弈的问题，并强调了多层验证、自动化检查机制在AI代码重建管线中的关键作用，但其有效性本身也面临验证机制未运行时的失效风险。

Abstract: An AI agent's rebuild is only as good as the process that produced it. Prior work found that once a model is strong enough, a multi-agent rebuild pipeline loses to the simplest approach: giving the model the original code and one instruction (AgentModernize). We present rebuild-dossier, an open-source tool that locks an application's real interface - its exact inputs and outputs - before any code is written, then enforces one-test-at-a-time building through automated checks, not written instructions alone.
  Three results shape this evaluation, with differing amounts of evidence. First, in a small comparison, the compliant agent failed a held-back test while the rule-breaking agent passed everything - proof that a passing suite doesn't certify correctness when tests can be gamed. Second, we tested whether this beats simply giving the weaker model the source and one instruction: tied on a small app, but lost outright on a larger one where the automated check wasn't even running - pointing to the check mechanism, not interface-locking, which held up separately. Third, every claim here is checked at three levels - the agent's own report, an automated log, and the actual files produced - catching real errors, including a bug in our own logging code, that a single level would have missed.
  These risks reproduce on a different model and toolchain: a stronger model followed our process three times running, something the weaker model never managed. The tool is public, MIT licensed, and reproduces end to end against our own applications.

</details>


### [155] [Identifying Latent Declarative Representations of Code for Assisting Repository Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23619)
*Shraddha Surana,Ashwin Srinivasan,Michael Bain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ADFD-Migrate方法，通过将程序表示为可注释的数据流图（ADFD）作为显式语义瓶颈，结合LLM和静态分析，实现仓库级软件迁移。在包含50个Fortran仓库的新基准f2x50上，生成Python代码通过85.6%的测试探针，迁移覆盖率和完整性显著优于直接翻译方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留软件仓库包含大量缺乏文档的领域知识，使得理解和现代化改造非常困难。作者希望探究将程序实现背后的潜在声明式描述显式化，是否能够改善仓库级代码移植的效果。

Method: ADFD-Migrate通过三步实现：1）用LLM从有界仓库上下文中推断源ADFD（包含进程、数据存储、外部实体、流和行为契约），并由静态分析覆盖检查引导；2）依赖感知的分块（dependency-aware chunking）对有限进程组排序以生成目标语言代码；3）比较源ADFD与静态恢复的目标ADFD，差异用于指导重新生成。

Result: 在f2x50基准（50个Fortran仓库，1.5k-160万行代码）上，382个测试探针中Python移植通过327个（85.6%），40个仓库全部探针通过。所有382个计划行为均可运行，远优于直接翻译（99/98）和消融实验（69/30）。平均迁移结果指数93.1%，在47个仓库上比直接翻译高17-59个百分点。

Conclusion: 可检查的语义瓶颈（ADFD）能够提高仓库级迁移的覆盖率和集成度，同时为许多仓库实现更低成本的代码生成，表明将潜在声明式表示显式化是提升大型软件迁移的有效途径。

Abstract: Legacy software repositories embed decades of domain knowledge in undocumented code, making understanding and modernization difficult. We treat a program as the implementation of an unobserved, declarative description of its computation and investigate whether making this latent declarative representation explicit improves repository-scale porting. ADFD-Migrate approximates the latent representation with an annotated data-flow diagram (ADFD) of processes, data stores, external entities, flows, and behavioral contracts. An LLM infers the source ADFD from bounded repository context, guided by static-analysis coverage checks. Dependency-aware chunking orders bounded process groups for target-language generation. Differences between the source ADFD and a statically recovered target ADFD then guide regeneration. We evaluate ADFD-Migrate on f2x50, a new benchmark of 50~Fortran repositories spanning 1.5k--1.6M lines of code and three complexity tiers, and assess the resulting ports along two dimensions: porting soundness, measured by source-oracle behavioral agreement, and porting completeness, measured by a composite migration outcome index. Against 382 curated Fortran-oracle probes, the generated Python passes 327 (85.6\%), with 40 repositories passing every attempted probe. ADFD-Migrate exposes all 382 planned behaviors as runnable targets, compared with 99 and 98 for direct and repository-context translation and 69 and 30 for the static-profile and dependency-chunking ablations. It also achieves a 93.1\% mean migration outcome index and a 17--59 percentage-point outcome-index advantage over direct translation on 47 repositories. These results suggest that an inspectable semantic bottleneck can improve the coverage and integration of repository-scale migration while enabling lower-cost generation for many repositories.

</details>


### [156] [When May an Agent Stop? Evidence-Carrying Termination for Tool-Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23623)
*Jason Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了"证据携带终止"(ECT)机制，要求智能代理在返回COMPLETE时必须绑定类型化证书、范围明确的追踪证据及确定性重放验证，从而在受控终止故障场景下显著降低不安全完成率（从252/288降至0/288），并在主测试集上实现支持完成率非劣效目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用智能代理需要决定何时终止任务，现有系统虽已具备终端成功门控、执行追踪认证或运行时策略，但未在COMPLETE边界上、结合受控终止故障、同时测试"收据-范围-封闭重放"这一特定设计。

Method: 提出证据携带终止(ECT)机制：代理返回COMPLETE时，类型化证书必须将每个必需答案声明绑定到有效的、范围内的追踪证据，并通过确定性重放重建声明值。在48个全合成任务、六类工具族、8种故障的锁定静态研究中评估，随后进行预注册的576轨迹研究，与终止评论核心、忠实控制器和全轨迹LLM评论者对比。

Result: 锁定静态研究中，ECT产生0/288不安全完成（对比基线252/288，差异-87.50 pp）；主测试集的22个保留任务簇上，ECT的0/66过早非支持终止对比控制器的40/66（差异-60.61 pp），支持完成率97/132对比92/132（差异3.79 pp），满足-10点非劣效边界。ECT成功恢复18/66轨迹，其中17条后续完成并得到支持，三个闭环门控全部通过。

Conclusion: ECT通过在记录轨迹中声明假设下认证支持（而非外部真实、安全或对齐性），能有效消除不安全的过早终止，同时在支持完成率上不劣于现有基线。

Abstract: Tool-using agents must decide when to stop. Existing systems already gate terminal success, certify execution traces, or enforce runtime polici es, but do not test this particular receipt-, scope-, and closed-replay design at the COMPLETE boundary across controlled termination faults. W e instantiate and evaluate Evidence-Carrying Termination (ECT): an agent may return COMPLETE only when a typed certificate binds every required answer claim to valid, in-scope trace evidence and a deterministic replay reconstructs the claimed value. A locked static study crosses 48 ful ly synthetic tasks in six tool-use families with clean execution and eight faults. ECT produced 0/288 unsafe completions versus 252/288 for the inspected termination-critic core (difference -87.50 pp, 95% task-cluster interval [-87.50, -87.50] pp). A fresh, prespecified and frozen 576- trajectory study then compares ECT with the critic core, its faithful controller, and a full-trace LLM critic. On 22 primary held-out task clus ters, ECT produced 0/66 premature unsupported terminations versus 40/66 for the controller (difference -60.61 pp, 95% interval [-78.79, -40.91] pp), while supported completion was 97/132 versus 92/132 (difference 3.79 pp, interval [0.00, 9.09] pp), satisfying a -10-point noninferiority margin. ECT executed successful recovery in 18/66 trajectories, of which 17 subsequently completed with support; all three closed-loop gates p assed. ECT certifies support in a recorded trace under declared assumptions, not external truth, safety, or alignment.

</details>


### [157] [Callability Is Not Operability: Controlled Interface Interventions for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23628)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究工具接口的"可操作性"（operability）与"可调用性"（callability）之间的差距，提出了Agent优先工具化（AFT）框架及AFT-Bench基准，通过受控的接口干预实验来评估不同工具接口设计对自主智能体在操作不确定性下继续安全执行能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使工具调用在语法上完全有效，自主智能体仍可能因缺乏足够的状态和语义信息而无法决定下一步行动。例如外部副作用已提交但响应丢失时，智能体无法区分已提交和未提交状态，尽管两者的后续操作不同。这种"可调用但不可操作"的差距会导致智能体在操作不确定性下陷入困境。

Method: 提出Agent-First Tooling（AFT），包含选择性能力发现、执行生命周期与恢复、显式外部效果语义、机器可读结果和后置条件验证等接口机制。同时构建AFT-Bench，这是一种受控的接口干预框架，在保持任务、后端、初始状态、注入故障、智能体和语言模型固定的前提下，仅改变暴露给智能体的工具接口。

Result: 论文未在摘要中给出具体实验结果，但AFT-Bench框架通过隔离变量（仅改变接口）能够系统性地量化不同工具接口设计对智能体在操作不确定性下继续安全执行能力的影响。

Conclusion: 工具设计应从"可调用"转向"可操作"，即工具接口需要暴露与动作相关的状态和语义，使智能体在操作不确定性下（如响应丢失但副作用已提交）仍能安全地决定并执行后续操作。

Abstract: A tool call can be perfectly valid yet still leave an autonomous agent unable to determine what to do next. For example, if an external effect commits but its response is lost, committed and uncommitted states may become indistinguishable to the agent even though they require different continuation actions. We study this gap between callability and operability: whether a tool interface exposes the action-relevant state and semantics needed for an agent to continue safely under operational uncertainty. We operationalize tool operability through Agent-First Tooling (AFT), a set of interface mechanisms spanning selective capability discovery, execution lifecycle and recovery, explicit external-effect semantics, machine-readable results, and postcondition verification. We introduce AFT-Bench, a controlled interface-intervention framework that holds the task, backend, initial state, injected failure, agent, and language model fixed while varying the interface exposed to the agent.

</details>


### [158] [FPGAgent: An LLM-Assisted Framework for Autonomous HLS Code Generation and Verification in FPGA Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23630)
*Tianyu Wang,Wenjie Wang,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出FPGAgent，一个面向真实FPGA环境的HLS代码生成多智能体框架，首次在公开基准上实现从任务描述到可执行设计的端到端验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based HLS代码生成方法仅验证仿真或综合结果，忽略了时序和布局布线约束，导致通过仿真和综合的代码仍可能无法在真实FPGA平台上部署运行；同时缺乏公开基准限制了评估规模。

Method: FPGAgent采用多智能体框架，包含三个关键环节：注入HLS特定知识并进行进化搜索以迭代生成可靠的HLS内核实现；生成C++验证程序以验证功能正确性并诊断缺陷进行定向修复；综合生成宿主代码用于编译和板级执行。

Result: 在包含78个跨领域任务的HLS-Eval基准上，使用五个主流LLM评估，并在真实FPGA平台验证板级可执行性。相比现有基线，综合率提升16.9%，可执行性提升26.7%，功能正确性提升30.6%。

Conclusion: FPGAgent显著提高了LLM生成HLS代码的实际可用性，验证了端到端验证对于保障真实FPGA可部署性的重要价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown substantial promise for high-level synthesis (HLS) code generation, but most existing approaches validate only simulation or synthesis results. Because of timing and place-and-route constraints, \emph{HLS code that passes simulation and synthesis may still fail to produce deployable, runnable designs on real FPGA platforms}. Moreover, the lack of public benchmarks has limited many evaluations to small, self-curated test suites. We propose FPGAgent, a multi-agent framework tailored to real FPGA environments for autonomous HLS coding with end-to-end executability validation. To the best of our knowledge, FPGAgent is \emph{the first task-specification-to-executable HLS generation framework experimentally validated on a well-established benchmark}. Given a natural-language task specification, FPGAgent injects HLS-specific knowledge and employs evolutionary search to iteratively derive reliable HLS kernel implementations. It then generates a C++ validation program to verify functional correctness, diagnoses potential defects, and guides targeted repairs. Finally, it synthesizes host code for compilation and board-level execution on FPGA hardware. We comprehensively evaluate FPGAgent with five established LLMs on HLS-Eval, a benchmark containing 78 tasks across multiple domains, and verify board-level executability on a real FPGA platform. Compared with existing baselines, FPGAgent improves the synthesizable rate by 16.9% on average, executability by 26.7%, and functional correctness by 30.6%. These results show that FPGAgent substantially improves the practical usability of LLM-based HLS generation and demonstrates the value of end-to-end validation.

</details>


### [159] [ToolRobustBench: Stage-Wise Perturbation Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis for Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23635)
*YiShan Zheng,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolRobustBench，一个面向工具调用智能体的阶段式诊断基准，通过四类扰动（工具接口、用户意图、工具输出/观察、运行时环境）系统性地归因工具调用流水线各阶段的失败原因，实验表明工具输出/观察扰动是主要瓶颈，并发现混合扰动存在不可叠加的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端（E2E）评估只能衡量工具调用的整体成功与否，无法定位失败发生在工具调用的哪个环节，也无法追踪错误如何在调用链中传播。这种黑盒式的评估方式阻碍了对工具调用智能体鲁棒性的深入理解和针对性改进。

Method: 构建ToolRobustBench阶段式诊断基准，将四类扰动家族（工具接口、用户意图、工具输出/观察、运行时环境）与工具调用流水线各阶段对齐，实现对工具选择、模式接地、参数绑定、工具输出/运行时反馈处理及端到端任务成功的细粒度归因。实验覆盖15,456个单家族实例、7个模型、16个本地工具、4个扰动家族和14个子类型。

Result: 模型在干净环境下的工具调用性能较高但不均匀，鲁棒性出现显著退化；其中工具输出/观察扰动是最大的鲁棒性瓶颈。混合家族实验揭示了单家族结果无法解释的非叠加性失败模式。

Conclusion: ToolRobustBench提供了一个确定性的、级联感知的基准，用于诊断工具调用智能体在干净精度之外的鲁棒性问题，有助于定位工具调用失败的根源及其传播路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on tool calling as a fundamental agent capability, enabling them to invoke external systems and complete tasks beyond text generation. However, clean end-to-end (E2E) success cannot identify where a tool-use failure originates or how it propagates through a call. We introduce ToolRobustBench, a stage-wise diagnostic benchmark for tool-calling agents, where a tool-calling agent is an LLM system that selects a tool, supplies structured arguments, and interprets its returned feedback. ToolRobustBench aligns four perturbation families with the tool-use pipeline: tool-interface, user-intent, tool-output/observation, and runtime-environment perturbations. It attributes failures to tool selection, schema grounding, argument binding, tool-output/runtime-feedback handling, and E2E task success. Experiments on 15,456 single-family instances across 7 models, 16 sampled local tools, 4 perturbation families, and 14 subtypes show high but non-uniform clean performance and substantial robustness degradation, with tool-output/observation perturbation the dominant bottleneck. Mixed-family experiments reveal non-additive failure patterns that are not explained by isolated single-family results. Thus, ToolRobustBench provides a deterministic and cascade-aware benchmark for diagnosing robustness beyond clean tool-calling accuracy;

</details>


### [160] [Feedback That Backfires: Why Small Language Model Agents Repeat the Call They Just Watched Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23651)
*Esmail Gumaan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了Agent系统中记录失败工具调用及其错误信息对模型行为的影响。作者通过量化"纠正增益"（失败记录导致模型重新发出失败动作的对数概率变化），发现所有测试的指令微调模型（4个家族，6个检查点，135M-1.7B参数）在模拟工具调用和MBPP程序修复两个环境中均表现出负增益。失败记录不仅没有帮助纠正，反而使模型更倾向于重复失败动作。作者进一步通过反事实实验分离出两个效应：失败调用的表面形式占83%的损害，语义标记的影响较小且不稳定。问题出在harness设计而非模型理解能力上，并提出了有效的补救措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent系统普遍假设在transcript中记录失败的工具调用及其错误信息可以作为一种纠正性反馈，帮助模型继续正确执行。作者质疑这一假设是否成立，希望通过量化手段检验失败记录是否真的提供了纠正性信息，以及如果无效，问题的根源在哪里、如何修复。

Method: 作者定义了"纠正增益"——失败记录导致模型重新发出刚失败调用的对数概率变化，作为衡量失败记录效用的指标。实验覆盖4个指令微调模型家族（135M-1.7B参数），在模拟工具调用和MBPP程序修复两个环境中测试。通过反事实实验（配对相同调用与失败消息、成功消息、中性确认）分离表面形式与语义贡献，并测试多种补救措施（运行时生成失败描述、解码器禁止重复、显式"不要重复"指令、删除失败尝试）。

Result: 核心发现：失败记录的纠正增益在所有测试模型和环境上均为负值（每个动作token约-1.03 nats，对应每个token 2.8倍的几率降低），且90%-100%的样本呈现此效应。重复失败调用的概率从0.06升至0.54，贪心解码在19%的样本上逐token重现失败调用（失败前为0%）。表面形式贡献83%的损害，语义贡献小且符号不一致。用运行时生成的失败描述替代逐字调用可移除76%的反转；使失败字符串在解码器不可达也有效。而显式"不要重复"指令无效，删除失败尝试（标准做法）是最差的方案。整个实验可在CPU上端到端运行。

Conclusion: 失败记录在Agent harness中的标准使用方式适得其反——记录逐字的失败调用本身会诱导模型重复该失败，而非从中学习。这个问题不在模型的错误理解能力，而在harness将失败调用以原始形式暴露给模型的方式。有效的修复方案是改变失败调用的表示形式（运行时描述）或限制其可生成性，而非依赖指令约束或删除历史记录。作者发布了全部代码和artefacts供复现。

Abstract: Agent harnesses record a failed tool call and its error message in the transcript and ask the model to continue, on the assumption that the error is corrective information. We measure whether it is. Defining the corrective gain of a failure record as the change in log-probability of re-emitting the action that just failed, we find the gain is negative for every instruction-tuned model we tested (6 checkpoints, 135M-1.7B, 4 families) in two environments: simulated tool calling and MBPP program repair. Normalised by action length the effect is about -1.03 nats per action token, a factor of 2.8 in the odds of each token, and holds on 90%-100% of individual items, not only on average. Over a fixed candidate set the probability of repeating the failed call rises from 0.06 to 0.54, and greedy decoding reproduces it token for token on 19% of items after the failure versus 0% before. Counterfactuals pairing the same call with a failure message, a success message, or a neutral acknowledgement separate two effects: the failed call's surface form accounts for 83% of the damage, while the semantic contribution of marking it failed is small and inconsistent in sign across environments. The problem is in the harness, not the model's grasp of error messages, and that predicts which remedies work. Replacing the verbatim call with a runtime-generated description of the failure removes 76% of the inversion at no token cost, and making previously-failed strings unreachable at the decoder acts on the same term. Two plausible remedies do not: an explicit "do not repeat" instruction leaves the measured quantity where it was, and deleting the failed attempt to retry from a clean context, the standard prescription for context contamination, is the worst harness we measured for repetition, because it restores the context that produced the failure. The study runs end to end on a CPU; all artefacts are released.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Executable Models: The Pufibara Agent Harness and the Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark for Physical System Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23653)
*Zizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Pufibara，一个用于Modelica物理系统建模的AI智能体框架，通过维护持久工程状态、关联仿真证据与候选版本、将提交作为显式动作来解决仿真驱动工程中需求丢失和证据过时的问题，并构建了232任务的Modelica智能体工作流基准，实验表明在匹配LLM后端的情况下，Pufibara在任务成功率和资源效率上均优于Claude Code。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统建模与通用代码生成不同，其正确性不仅取决于语法和可执行性，还取决于物理一致性和场景依赖行为。在Modelica等基于方程的建模语言中，模型可能编译和仿真成功但仍违反预期的物理或工程需求。智能体在多次修订中可能丢失需求或依赖过时候选版本产生的仿真证据，这些问题需要专门的解决方案。

Method: 提出Pufibara智能体框架，包含三个关键设计：跨修订维护持久工程状态、将执行和仿真证据与产生它的候选版本关联、将提交作为显式的智能体动作。同时提出一种基于源构建现实且可独立评估任务的方法，据此构建包含232个任务的Modelica智能体工作流基准，涵盖模型修复、模型生成和模型调优三种任务类型，由基准拥有的评估器在智能体循环外打分。

Result: 与Claude Code作为完整框架在两种匹配的LLM后端下对比：使用DeepSeek v4 Flash时，Pufibara通过202个任务（Claude Code为185个）；使用Claude Sonnet 5时，Pufibara通过202个任务（Claude Code为187个）。Pufibara的逻辑token总量降低76.4%-82.5%，顺序运行时间降低6.1%-58.4%。

Conclusion: 即使在匹配的LLM后端下，完整的智能体框架在物理系统建模的任务成功率和资源使用上可能存在显著差异。Pufibara通过持久状态管理、证据关联和显式提交机制，显著提升了Modelica建模任务的成功率和资源效率。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for simulation-driven engineering. Physical system modeling presents different requirements from general-purpose code generation in software engineering, because correctness depends not only on syntax and executability but also on physical consistency and scenario-dependent behavior. We study this challenge in Modelica, an equation-based modeling language in which a model may compile and simulate while still violating its intended physics or engineering requirements. Across successive revisions, an agent may lose track of requirements or rely on simulation evidence produced by an outdated candidate.
  To address this challenge, we present Pufibara, an agent harness that maintains persistent engineering state across revisions, associates execution and simulation evidence with the candidate that produced it, and makes submission an explicit agent action. To evaluate end-to-end Modelica agent workflows, we also propose a source-grounded method for constructing realistic and independently evaluable tasks. We use this method to build the 232-task Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark, spanning Model Repair, Model Generation, and Model Tuning. Each submitted candidate is scored by a benchmark-owned evaluator outside the agent loop.
  We compare Pufibara with Claude Code as complete harnesses under two matched large language model (LLM) backends. With DeepSeek v4 Flash, Pufibara passes 202 tasks, compared with 185 for Claude Code. With Claude Sonnet 5, Pufibara passes 202 tasks, compared with 187 for Claude Code. Under the repository-reported token accounting, Pufibara records 76.4%-82.5% lower logical-token totals. Its sequential runtime is 6.1%-58.4% lower. These findings show that, even under matched LLM backends, complete agent harnesses can differ substantially in both task success and resource use for physical system modeling.

</details>


### [162] [Confidently Wrong, Silently So: Auditing Undetectable Failures of a Deployed On-Device Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23663)
*Shashwat Pandey,Satwik Pandey,Suresh Raghu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对端侧部署的语言模型进行可复现的可靠性审计，发现其存在"任务不对称的校准错误"：在69%的虚假前提问题上产生自信幻觉，同时拒绝18%的良性输入；自报置信度饱和且无判别力（AUROC 0.47）。自信正确与自信错误的输出在表面特征上难以区分（AUROC仅0.55），但无需模型访问权限的黑盒一致性包装器可将自信幻觉从75%降至3%，选择性准确率从43%提升至83%。作者发布了模型无关的审计协议、表面不可区分性测试、代码和冻结的评估项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着端侧模型部署到数亿设备且缺乏服务端审核，独立审计开发者可配置的模型可靠性成为一个关键但未被充分研究的监督问题——用户和资源受限的开发者需要知道模型何时出错。

Method: 对开发者可访问的端侧基础模型进行红队测试，涵盖校准、虚假前提问题上的自信幻觉、良性输入的过度拒绝三类测试；并构建表面不可区分性测试来检验自信正确与自信错误输出能否通过15个用户可见特征区分；同时测试单次生成信号与黑盒一致性包装器两种监控方案。

Result: 发现任务不对称的校准错误（69%虚假前提幻觉 vs 18%良性输入拒绝）；自报置信度非判别性（AUROC 0.47，ECE 70，在同类小模型中表现最差）；15个用户可见特征只能达到AUROC 0.55的区分度；单次生成信号最高仅0.68 AUROC；而黑盒一致性包装器显著恢复可靠性（幻觉75%→3%，选择性准确率43%→83%）。

Conclusion: 部署模型的可靠性监控不能依赖单一或表面信号；在推理时没有廉价信号可以标记失败，但模型无关的审计协议和黑盒一致性包装器提供了可复现、可扩展的监督手段，为审计已部署模型提供了可复用基础设施。

Abstract: Aligning deployed language models requires knowing when their outputs can be trusted, yet on-device models now ship to hundreds of millions of devices with no server-side moderation, and the configuration developers can actually deploy is rarely audited independently. We present a reproducible reliability audit of the developer-accessible on-device foundation model, framed as an oversight question: can a user or a resource-constrained developer tell when the model is wrong? Red-teaming it on calibration, confident confabulation on false-premise questions, and over-refusal of benign prompts, we find a \emph{task-asymmetric miscalibration}: its guardrails fail in opposite directions across tasks (confabulating on 69\% of false premises while refusing 18\% of entirely benign inputs), atop a self-reported confidence that is saturated and non-discriminative (AUROC 0.47; ECE 70, worst among comparable small models). Crucially, confident-correct and confident-wrong outputs are \emph{surface-indistinguishable}: a classifier over 15 user-visible features separates them at AUROC only 0.55 (equivalence-confirmed), leaving no signal for oversight at inference time. No cheap single-generation signal flags these failures ($\le$0.68 AUROC), whereas a black-box consistency wrapper requiring no model access recovers reliability (confident confabulation 75\%$\to$3\%; selective accuracy 43\%$\to$83\%) at a tunable cost. We contribute a model-agnostic audit protocol, a surface-indistinguishability test, and released code and frozen evaluation items as reusable infrastructure for auditing deployed models.

</details>


### [163] [Enhancing Bug Report Templates in the TianoCore UEFI Firmware Development Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23754)
*Laura Baird,Neelesh Reddybattula,Nazanin Siavash,Terrance E. Boult,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出增强TianoCore开源社区GitHub Issues中的缺陷报告模板，通过添加新字段来改进缺陷分类和解决流程，目前处于进行中阶段，已对少数开发者进行了访谈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TianoCore社区的缺陷报告中，对缺陷分类和修复有用的信息有时只在自由文本中偶尔出现，缺乏结构化字段，导致bug triage和修复效率不高。

Method: 分析缺陷仓库数据以发现对bug triage和修复有用的信息模式，在现有TianoCore缺陷报告模板中添加几个新字段，并通过开发者访谈和A/B测试进行验证。

Result: 目前仅完成了初步的数据分析、模板字段设计以及少数开发者的访谈反馈，验证工作仍处于进行中。

Conclusion: 计划通过更多TianoCore社区访谈和A/B测试来验证所提出的增强缺陷报告模板方法，以实现更有效和高效的bug triage与解决。

Abstract: We propose enhancing the bug report templates in the GitHub Issues issue tracking system used by the TianoCore open-source community with the aim of improving the bug triage and resolution process. We analyze the bug repository data and find patterns of information that are useful for bug triage and fixing. However, some of them are only occasionally included in the free-form text of bug reports. Therefore, we propose adding a few new fields to the existing TianoCore bug report template. In this study, we focus on the key TianoCore project, EDK II, which constitutes the core of the UEFI firmware across various firmware vendors and original equipment manufacturers. This study is currently a work-in-progress. So far, we have interviewed a few developers to obtain their feedback and adjust the proposed approach. We are planning more interviews with the TianoCore community to conduct A/B tests and validate our approach to achieve effective and efficient bug triage and resolution.

</details>


### [164] [SPIDER4TianoCore: Enhancing Patch-Propagation for the TianoCore UEFI Firmware Development Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23755)
*Laura Baird,Devin Haggitt,Terrance E. Boult,Aravind Machiry,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SPIDER4TianoCore是一个Python命令行工具，用于在TianoCore/UEFI固件供应链中提供集成阶段补丁状态证据。该工具对上游补丁前后配对与下游目标进行分析，报告漏洞、已修补、不适用或不确定四种状态，并为维护者审查提供支持证据。在20个目标/CVE配对上的评估显示，分析器产生了10个高置信度的补丁前匹配和4个高置信度的补丁后匹配，对6个目标保守弃权，没有做出与人工标注相矛盾的错误分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UEFI固件供应链中，补丁从上游传播到下游集成阶段时，缺乏自动化工具来提供补丁状态的证据支持，维护者难以快速判断下游目标是否已修补、仍存在漏洞或无需修补，因此需要一种工具来生成集成阶段的补丁状态证据。

Method: 该方法基于SPIDER的补丁传播框架，构建一个打包的Python命令行工具。给定上游补丁前/后代码对和准备好的下游目标，通过分析器对目标进行匹配分析，输出Vulnerable（漏洞）、Already Patched（已修补）、Not Applicable（不适用）或Uncertain（不确定）四种状态，并附带支持证据供维护者审查。该工具不证明补丁可以安全传播，仅提供证据辅助决策。

Result: 在来自八个公开下游EDK II仓库的两个CVE、20个目标/CVE配对上的评估中，分析器产生10个高置信度的补丁前匹配和4个高置信度的补丁后匹配，对6个目标保守弃权，相对于人工记录的手动补丁状态标签，没有高置信度的错误分类。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明，SPIDER4TianoCore能够为准备好的目标生成可复现的证据，而非展示对一般下游的通用准确性。该工具有助于UEFI固件供应链维护者在集成阶段获得补丁状态证据支持，但仍需进一步改进以扩展其适用范围。

Abstract: We propose and demonstrate SPIDER4TianoCore, a packaged Python command-line tool that provides integration-stage patch-status evidence for the TianoCore/UEFI firmware supply chain. Given an upstream pre-patch and post-patch pair and prepared downstream targets, the tool reports Vulnerable, Already Patched, Not Applicable, or Uncertain with supporting evidence for maintainer review. Our work is inspired by SPIDER's patch-propagation framing, but SPIDER4TianoCore does not itself prove that a patch is safe to propagate. We evaluate the engine on 20 prepared target/CVE pairs from eight public downstream EDK II repositories and two CVEs. The analyzers produce 10 high-confidence pre-patch matches and four high-confidence post-patch matches, conservatively abstain on six targets, and make no confidently wrong classifications relative to the recorded manual patch-state labels. These preliminary results demonstrate reproducible evidence generation for prepared targets rather than general downstream accuracy.

</details>


### [165] [Scale, Concentration, and Entry Timing in the Shopify App Ecosystem: A Longitudinal Study of Platform Governance and Application Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23771)
*Fabrizio Assabese,Peter-Jan Celis,Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文利用2025年9月Shopify平台上24,826个应用的全量快照、7,708个应用366周的周度面板数据及Internet Archive重构的上市历史，分析了应用市场的结构、采用驱动因素与失败规律。研究发现多数类别低集中度、后来者增长快于先驱者、平台治理（如Shopify自营应用进入与退出）不对称地重塑了竞争格局，且应用失败可通过早期公开数据高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Shopify生态规模巨大（16,000+活跃应用、270万商户、估计7,060亿美元年销售额），但缺乏对其市场结构和采用驱动因素的实证研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用多重数据源：2025年9月全部24,826个应用快照、7,708个应用366周（2019年2月至2026年3月）的周度面板、Internet Archive重构的上市历史；结合集中度分析、进入时序效应、平台治理事件（自营进入、产品下架、收入分成调整）和机器学习预测（AUC）等方法。

Result: 55个功能类别中半数以上低集中度、20%高度集中；88%的类别中后来者增速快于先驱者；Shopify进入聊天领域逆转了两年多的去集中化趋势；Product Reviews下架释放了191,000个安装但最多仅三分之一被竞争对手承接；收入分成下调无显著影响；中位退出应用峰值仅8个安装、68周内消失；应用前六个月数据预测两年内退出的交叉验证AUC超过0.8。

Conclusion: Shopify应用市场具有高度可竞争性，获益者更多取决于平台治理与进入条件而非进入时机；失败大多无声但可预测，平台生态应用的生死格局主要由治理决策塑造。

Abstract: The Shopify marketplace hosts more than 16,000 active third-party applications, serving 2.7 million active merchant stores generating an estimated \$706 billion in annual sales, yet little empirical evidence exists on its structure and adoption drivers. We analyse it using a September 2025 snapshot of all 24,826 applications, a weekly panel of 7,708 applications over 366 weeks (February 2019 to March 2026), and listing histories reconstructed from the Internet Archive. Across 55 established functional categories, over half were low-concentration and 20\% highly concentrated, with larger categories consistently less concentrated. Later entrants grew faster than early movers in 88\% of the 50 analysed categories, persisting across seven years of quarterly re-estimations, with early movers on net losing detected installations whilst late movers gained them. Platform governance reshaped competition asymmetrically: Shopify's entry into chat reversed more than two years of de-concentration; the deprecation of its Product Reviews application, to our knowledge the first measured platform-owner exit from complementor category, released 191,000 installations of which at most a third reappeared as competitor adoption within a year; the 2021 reduction of the platform revenue share produced no detectable change in entry or retention. Failure is largely silent and predictable: the median exiting application peaked at 8 detected installations and disappeared from tracking within 68 weeks of launch, category leadership changed hands in 92\% of categories over seven years, and publicly observable data from an application's first six months predict exit within two years with cross-validated AUC above 0.8. App markets on Shopify remain contestable; who benefits depends on platform governance and entry conditions more than on entry timing.

</details>


### [166] [Automated Synthesis of Cloud Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23842)
*Archit Bhatnagar,Zhenning Yang,Sarah McClure,Yiming Qiu,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Ang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CloudEmu是一种自动化方法，通过神经符号代码合成从云文档构建云模拟器，结合LLM的理解能力与云特定的符号抽象来抑制幻觉，并以真实云作为oracle进行自动化测试和修复。实验表明在AWS和GCP上优于手工开发的LocalStack。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DevOps编程测试需要在真实云资源上执行，耗时、不安全且成本高。云模拟器虽能缓解此问题，但手工构建模拟器需要开发者手动解读大量云文档并为每个服务、API及其交互手工编写逻辑，难以扩展到云的复杂性和持续演进的API。

Method: CloudEmu采用神经符号代码合成方法：利用LLM在文档理解和代码生成方面的通用能力，结合云特定的符号抽象来抑制幻觉并保证精度，同时使用真实云作为oracle进行自动化测试、修复和对齐。

Result: 在AWS和GCP的主要云服务上，CloudEmu在覆盖率和准确性方面均表现出有效性，并超越了由大型工程师团队手工开发十余年的现有领先工具LocalStack。

Conclusion: 基于文档自动合成云模拟器是可行且有效的，神经符号方法能够替代大规模手工开发，为DevOps程序测试提供更高效、更可靠的模拟环境。

Abstract: DevOps programming (e.g., using CLI/API scripts or IaC frameworks) is key to cloud infrastructure management. Unlike traditional programming tasks, DevOps program testing needs provisioning and execution against actual cloud resources, which is often time-consuming, unsafe, and costly. Cloud emulators have gained popularity for easing DevOps program testing; they are generally API-level mocks that can execute DevOps programs in a local environment. Still, building these emulators remains challenging: developers must manually interpret extensive cloud documentation and handcraft logic for each service, API, and their interaction. This does not scale to the complexity of the cloud, which is further a moving target as the services and APIs evolve. CloudEmu is an automated approach that constructs emulators based on cloud documentation via neurosymbolic code synthesis. The key idea is to combine LLMs' general strengths in documentation understanding and code generation with cloud-specific symbolic abstractions that suppress hallucinations and enforce precision at scale, while using the real cloud as an oracle for automated testing, repair, and alignment. Our evaluation shows the effectiveness of CloudEmu on major cloud provider (AWS and GCP) services in both coverage and accuracy. CloudEmu outperforms the existing leading tool LocalStack, which was manually developed by a large team of engineers over a decade.

</details>


### [167] [The Empire, Long Divided, Must Unite: Architectural Convergence in Three LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23953)
*Dai Jiahong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过源代码级多案例研究，分析了三个采用不同设计哲学的开源编码Agent框架（deepagents、pi、dsh），发现两个成熟框架虽走了相反路线却收敛于同一架构中间形态（五个重复出现的要素），并通过留出验证确认了这种收敛，同时指出外部可验证性这一关键维度的缺失是下一个差异化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent框架（harness）作为将语言模型转化为自主智能体的外围代码层，正日益成为制约智能体行为的关键瓶颈，而非模型本身。然而目前缺乏对这个关键层的系统研究，尤其是不同设计哲学下框架结构的演化规律的深入理解。

Method: 采用源代码级、多案例的分析方法，选择三个设计理念对立的开源Agent框架（batteries-included的deepagents、激进极简主义的pi、一切皆插件的dsh），在每个固定的commit上精读源代码并追踪其commit历史，同时将第三个框架作为留出验证样本。

Result: 两个成熟框架虽然演化方向相反（deepagents删减作者编写的脚手架，pi积累持久化基础设施），但都收敛到包含五个重复要素的架构中间形态：商品化的循环、可追加回放的会话记录、作为数据保存的模型怪癖、上下文的渐进式披露、显式扩展缝隙。留出验证的第三个框架也具备全部五个要素，且在一个缝隙中直接复用了其他框架的实现。收敛可分解为平行发现、扩散和字面复用三种机制。此外，外部可验证性（无需信任运行时的防篡改记录）在所有框架中均缺失。

Conclusion: Agent框架架构的收敛揭示了该领域的共同设计约束，而与模型无关的框架层正成为智能体行为的关键决定因素。外部可验证性的缺失不应被视为疏忽，而是预测性的空白，将是面向溯源敏感领域框架的下一个差异化竞争轴心。

Abstract: An agent harness is what turns a language model into an autonomous agent: the surrounding code that builds the model's context, mediates its tools, runs the loop, and persists state across a long-horizon run. This layer, not the model it wraps, is increasingly the binding constraint on agent behaviour. We present a source-level, multi-case study of three open coding-agent harnesses built from deliberately opposing philosophies: LangChain's deepagents (batteries-included), Earendil's pi (radical minimalism), and DeepSeek's dsh (everything-is-a-plugin). Reading each at a pinned commit and following its commit history, we find that the two mature harnesses have travelled in opposite directions (deepagents subtracting authored scaffolding, pi accreting durable infrastructure), yet converged toward one architectural middle form of five recurring elements: a commoditised loop, an append-only replayable session record, model quirks kept as data, progressive disclosure of context, and explicit extension seams. A third harness, read afterward as a held-out check, exhibits all five, and in one seam reuses another's implementation outright. We therefore do not claim independent invention, and decompose the convergence into parallel discovery, diffusion, and literal reuse. Finally, one load-bearing dimension shows no convergence, and indeed no presence: external verifiability, a tamper-evident record an outside party can check without trusting the runtime. We read this absence not as an oversight but as a predictive gap, the next axis on which harnesses for provenance-sensitive domains will differ.

</details>


### [168] [Evaluating Language Models on Cross-Language Code Functional Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23961)
*Hui Sun,Anderson Uchôa,Rohit Gheyi,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PolyHuman数据集（CPP、Java、Python三种语言的人类编写程序），系统评估了多种LLM在跨语言和同语言功能等价性判断上的表现，发现模型存在难度依赖性的误判倾向、对特定语言的敏感性（如Python上更保守）、依赖表面相似性线索、以及GPT-o4-mini在相同设置下的显著运行不稳定性，结论是当前LLM不能可靠地捕获程序功能等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码理解评估主要局限于单语言环境或合成代码，无法真实反映模型对程序语义的理解能力。作者旨在通过跨语言、人类编写的真实代码来检验LLM是否能准确判断程序功能等价性，这需要超越表面相似性的深层推理。

Method: 构建包含CPP、Java、Python人类编写程序的PolyHuman数据集，评估开源和闭源LLM在语言内和跨语言等价性检测任务上的表现；以GPT-o4-mini为代表模型分析稳定性；手动分析81个系统性错误案例，检查代码逻辑和Chain-of-Thought推理；将失败模式分类并在GPT-o4-mini、Claude-Opus-4.7和Gemini-3-Flash之间对比。

Result: 发现等价性判断存在难度依赖性退化（难题更易将不等价代码误判为等价）；最佳模型对编程语言有特定敏感性（尤其对Python表现更保守）；模型部分依赖相似性线索而非真实语义；GPT-o4-mini在相同设置下存在显著的运行间不稳定性，表明能力不一致而非完全缺失。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在语言内和跨语言场景下均不能可靠地判断程序功能等价性，其表现暗示模型更多依赖表面特征而非深层语义推理，这挑战了"LLM能够真正理解程序语义"的假设。

Abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a variety of code-understanding tasks, leading many to believe that they can reason about program semantics. However, existing evaluations primarily focus on single-language settings or rely on synthetically generated code, raising concerns about whether current results reflect true semantic understanding. Aims: We investigate whether LLMs can accurately judge functional equivalence across different programming languages in human-written code, a setting that requires deeper reasoning beyond superficial similarity. Method: We introduce PolyHuman, a dataset of human-written programs in CPP, Java, and Python. Using this dataset, we evaluate intra- and inter-language equivalence detection across open-weight and proprietary LLMs, selecting GPT-o4-mini as a representative model to assess stability. We then manually analyze 81 cases of systematic disagreement in which models incorrectly judge functional equivalence, examining the code logic and the generated Chain-of-Thought reasoning. Finally, we categorize these failures and compare them across GPT-o4-mini, Claude-Opus-4.7, and Gemini-3-Flash to determine whether they reflect model-specific issues or broader limitations of state-of-the-art LLMs. Results: We identify a difficulty-dependent breakdown in equivalence judgment (harder problems make the model increasingly prone to misclassifying non-equivalent code as equivalent), a model-specific sensitivity to programming language for the best-performing model (particularly a more conservative behavior on Python), and a partial reliance on similarity-based cues. GPT-o4-mini also shows substantial run-to-run instability under identical settings, indicating inconsistent rather than absent capability. Conclusions: Current LLMs do not reliably capture functional equivalence within or across languages.

</details>


### [169] [DeepRepoQA: Code Repository Question Answering with Deep Agent Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24221)
*Weihan Peng,Yuling Shi,Yingwei Ma,Longfei Yun,Beijun Shen,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepRepoQA，一种基于MCTS的智能体框架，用于仓库级代码理解的问题回答，在SWE-QA基准上显著超越现有强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仓库理解方法主要依赖表面级的代码检索，缺乏跨多文件、复杂软件架构下的深度推理能力，难以将答案扎根于长距离代码依赖中。

Method: DeepRepoQA采用智能体框架，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）机制使LLM智能体能够动态搜索、导航和检查代码，实现对长距离代码依赖的多跳推理。

Result: 在SWE-QA基准上的综合实验表明，DeepRepoQA相比强基线获得了显著的性能提升，验证了系统化MCTS引导探索的有效性。

Conclusion: MCTS引导的系统化探索能够有效解决多跳仓库级推理问题，为代码库复杂问题回答提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Answering developer questions about a software repository is a critical yet under-explored problem in software engineering. While existing repository understanding methods have advanced the field, they predominantly rely on surface-level code retrieval and lack the ability for deep reasoning over multiple files, complex software architectures, and grounding answers in long-range code dependencies. To address these limitations, we propose DeepRepoQA, a novel question answering (QA) framework for repository-level code understanding. DeepRepoQA builds on an agentic framework where LLM agents find answers through a systematic tree search over the repository structure. A Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) mechanism is employed to empower agents to dynamically search, navigate, and inspect code, enabling effective multi-hop reasoning over long-range code dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on the SWE-QA benchmark demonstrate substantial performance gains over strong baselines, validating the effectiveness of systematic MCTS-guided exploration for multi-hop repository reasoning.

</details>


### [170] [Mutation Testing of Simulink Cyber-Physical System Models: Challenges and Solutions in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24250)
*Murat Kavak,Onur Kilincceker,Serge Demeyer,Kevin Vandenbroucke,Halim Abdurrahman Ceylan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析了在DUCO公司（建筑通风系统制造商）的模型驱动工程环境中引入变异测试的挑战与经验教训，重点关注Simulink/StateFlow模型中的等价变异体、需求追溯性及Stateflow变异测试等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业界，后期缺陷发现成本极高，变异测试可支持左移测试策略。然而在网络物理系统中，需对设计模型进行测试。本文旨在探索将变异测试应用于工业模型驱动工程实践中的实际挑战。

Method: 在DUCO公司开展试点项目，使用工具原型MUT4SLX，在基于Simulink和StateFlow的模型工程环境中引入变异测试，分析实施过程中遇到的挑战并总结经验。

Result: 识别出三类尚未在学术文献中充分报道的特定挑战：等价变异体问题、需求可追溯性问题，以及Stateflow模型的变异测试难题。

Conclusion: 变异测试在工业模型驱动工程中的落地需要针对等价变异体、需求追溯和Stateflow特殊语义的专门研究，以实现左移测试在类似工业场景中的有效应用。

Abstract: Several experience reports illustrate that mutation testing is capable of supporting a shift-left testing strategy, especially in industries where late bug discovery incurs very high costs and risks. In the context of cyber-physical systems, a shift left implies that engineers need to test the design models used to simulate, prototype, and analyse the feasibility of the system under design. In this paper, we analyse the challenges we encountered and the lessons we learned when incorporating mutation testing in the context of DUCO, a company producing ventilation systems for buildings. The engineers within DUCO have years of experience with model-based engineering centered around Simulink and StateFlow, including unit tests for their models. During a pilot project with our tool prototype MUT4SLX we learned that equivalent mutants, requirement traceability, and mutation testing for Stateflow represent particular challenges not yet reported in the academic literature.

</details>


### [171] [Cross-Stack Validation of Language-Model Training: A Clinical Fine-Tuning Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24267)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轨迹级别的协议，利用独立实现的训练栈作为差分预言机来验证整个微调流水线。通过对LoRA适配Qwen3-0.6B模型在168,574条临床问答数据对上进行PyTorch和Zig编写的numbat框架（经C接口桥接六种语言）的交叉验证，发现了两栈间交叉熵仅相差0.134%，但暴露了17个单实现开发中遗漏的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络训练存在"预言机问题"：训练可以正常收敛并产出可用模型，但底层软件实际计算的内容可能与规范不符。几乎所有此类工作运行在同一技术栈上，缺乏独立验证手段。先前的差分测试仅针对算子和推理路径，缺少对完整训练流水线的验证。

Method: 定义轨迹级别协议：(1)共享规范；(2)跨检查点覆盖算术、模型加载、数据渲染和学习轨迹；(3)对技术栈、编排和语言运行时的独立性进行分离。将该协议应用于LoRA微调Qwen3-0.6B，在PyTorch和Zig编写的numbat框架下运行，并通过C接口驱动六种语言，进行42次配对评估覆盖完整训练周期。

Result: 两种技术栈的保留集交叉熵平均仅相差0.134%，四种实现在一个训练周期内彼此差距在0.15%以内。比较暴露了17个缺陷，其中对训练模型影响最大的缺陷不在数值内核中：临床文本渲染方式的不匹配使保留集损失移动了0.15，比数值缺陷大约500倍。另有4个缺陷仅能从内存模型与前两种实现不同的语言中触达（线程间迁移任务的调度器、对设备内存盲目的收集器、接口缺少的拥有权原语）。

Conclusion: 实现多样性具有多个维度，运行时是其中重要的一环。独立实现的训练栈可以作为整个微调流水线的差分预言机，其价值不仅限于数值内核层面的验证，还能揭示数据渲染和语言运行时差异引入的深层缺陷。

Abstract: Neural network training has an oracle problem: a run can converge normally and yield a usable model while the software beneath it computes something other than specified. Almost all such work runs on one stack, so there is rarely anything independent to check against. We study whether independently implemented training stacks can serve as differential oracles for a whole fine-tuning pipeline, rather than the operators and inference paths that prior differential testing targets. We define a trajectory-level protocol -- a shared specification, cross-check points spanning arithmetic, model loading, data rendering and the learning trajectory, and a separation of independence of the stack, the orchestration and the language runtime -- and apply it to a LoRA adaptation of Qwen3-0.6B over 168,574 clinical question-answer pairs under PyTorch and under numbat, an independent framework written in Zig, driven natively and through its C interface from six languages. Across 42 paired evaluations spanning a full epoch the two stacks' held-out cross-entropy differs by 0.134% on average, and four implementations end the epoch within 0.15% of one another. The comparison exposed 17 faults that single-implementation development had missed, two of them notable for software engineering.
