The fault with the largest effect on the trained model lay outside the numerical kernels: a mismatch in how clinical text was rendered moved held-out loss 0.15, some 500 times more than the arithmetic faults found beside it. And four faults were reachable only from a language whose memory model differs from the first two implementations: a scheduler migrating work across threads, a collector blind to device memory, an ownership discipline needing a primitive the interface lacked. Implementation diversity has several axes, and the runtime is one.

</details>


### [172] [Towards LLM-Enhanced Android Taint Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24269)
*Nicholas Miazzo,Marco Alecci,Jordan Samhi,Jacques Klein,Eleonora Losiouk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究使用现成的大语言模型（LLM）进行Android应用的污点分析。通过智能体交互策略让LLM迭代探索代码并推理数据流，在DroidBench基准上F1分数达0.96，远超FlowDroid的0.55，尤其在组件间通信、隐式流和反射等FlowDroid薄弱环节表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的静态污点分析工具（如FlowDroid）在准确建模Android框架方面面临众所周知的挑战，需要探索新的方法来提高污点分析的准确性和覆盖范围。

Method: 采用基于智能体交互策略的方法，让LLM（如Gemini-3 Flash）迭代地探索代码库并推理数据流，以判断Android应用中是否存在敏感数据泄露。

Result: 在DroidBench基准上，LLM方法F1分数达0.96，远高于FlowDroid的0.55。在组件间通信（0.95 vs 0.17）、隐式流（0.94 vs 0.00）和反射（1.00 vs 0.50）等挑战性类别中显著超越FlowDroid。在真实世界应用中也发现了FlowDroid未报告的数据泄露。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明LLM推理可以有效补充传统静态污点分析，为未来混合式LLM增强的污点分析流水线研究提供了动机和方向。

Abstract: Taint analysis is a fundamental technique for detecting sensitive data leaks in Android apps. However, traditional static tools, such as FlowDroid, still face well-known challenges due to the complexity of accurately modeling the Android framework. In this paper, we investigate whether off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can effectively reason about taint flows in Android apps. Our preliminary approach relies on an agentic interaction strategy, enabling the LLM to iteratively explore code and reason about data flows.
  We conduct an initial evaluation on the DroidBench benchmark against FlowDroid, where our approach outperforms the baseline: Gemini-3 Flash achieves an F1-score of 0.96, compared to 0.55 for FlowDroid. In particular, we observe improvements in challenging categories such as inter-component communication (0.95 vs. 0.17), implicit flows (0.94 vs. 0.00), and reflection (1.00 vs. 0.50), where FlowDroid typically struggles. On a small set of real-world apps, the LLM-based approach also identifies additional potential data leaks not reported by FlowDroid. These preliminary findings suggest that LLM reasoning may effectively complement traditional static taint analysis, motivating future research on hybrid LLM-enhanced taint analysis pipelines.

</details>


### [173] [Observability and Fault Injection for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24271)
*Zahra Seyedghorban,Egor Klimov,Arie van Deursen,Annibale Panichella,Burcu Kulahcioglu Ozkan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了llmmas-otel，一个轻量级且框架无关的工具，结合OpenTelemetry分布式追踪与故障注入，用于LLM多智能体软件工程任务的可观测性、调试和受控故障评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在软件工程任务中日益普及，但这些系统难以检查、调试和在受控故障条件下评估。

Method: 该工具通过OpenTelemetry在多个层面（工作流阶段、智能体步骤、智能体间通信、工具调用和LLM调用）对智能体执行进行追踪插桩，并支持在选定交互点进行针对性故障注入，使基线执行与故障执行可复现对比。

Result: 作者在最小演示工作流和真实的LLM多智能体软件开发系统上进行了初步验证。

Conclusion: llmmas-otel提供了可复现的机制来对比基线与故障执行，通过对齐的追踪和运行工件检查故障影响，但验证仍处于初期阶段。

Abstract: Large Language Model-based multi-agent systems are increasingly explored for software engineering tasks, but they remain difficult to inspect, debug, and evaluate under controlled failures. We present llmmas-otel, a lightweight and framework-agnostic tool that combines OpenTelemetry-based distributed tracing with fault injection for LLM-based multi-agent systems in software engineering workflows. The tool instruments agent executions with trace-aligned telemetry across workflow phases, agent steps, inter-agent communication, tool calls, and LLM invocations, and supports targeted fault injection at selected interaction points. This makes it possible to compare baseline and faulty executions in a reproducible way and inspect the effects through aligned traces and run artifacts. We describe the motivation, architecture, implementation, current capabilities, and initial validation of the tool on a minimal demo workflow and a real LLM-based multi-agent system for software development.

</details>


### [174] [Ockhamareto: Pareto-Gated Segment-Level Credit Assignment for Concise Unit-Test Generation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24473)
*Dong Huang,Mark Harman,Jie M. Zhang,Zhijiang Guo,Mingzhe Du,See Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Ockhamareto框架，基于Ockham剃刀和Pareto最优原理进行单次GRPO单元测试生成与选择，通过Pareto门控奖励和Token级段信用实现测试套件的效率与有效性最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL基线（如MIST-RL）生成的单元测试套件在突变分数（捕获bug能力）与测试数量（效率）之间存在次优权衡，缺乏系统性的Pareto最优性保障。此外，证明该权衡拐点无法用简单代理指标（如函数大小）预测，因此需要显式计算Pareto前沿。

Method: 包含两个核心组件：(i) Pareto门控奖励——仅奖励在(突变分数, -测试数量)空间中非支配的rollout；(ii) Token级段信用——将每个测试的边际突变击杀归因到其单元测试块的token上。采用单次GRPO框架，无需多次采样迭代。

Result: 在UnLeakedTestBench上严格Pareto支配最强RL基线MIST-RL：突变分数49.9% vs 31.3%（N=5），平均测试数2.60 vs 4.67，每测试权衡改进达3.4倍。在全部四个基准（HumanEval+、MBPP+、CodeContests、TestGenEval-Lite）上均领先突变与覆盖率指标且测试套件最小。在4B、9B、27B三种规模下均超越SOTA，突变提升+30-35个百分点。

Conclusion: Pareto前沿计算是必要的工程手段，用于识别每个被测函数的效率与有效性之间的关键权衡拐点，且该拐点无法通过简单代理指标预测。Ockhamareto证明了将Ockham剃刀原则与Pareto最优性结合能够同时提高测试发现bug的能力和效率。

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{Ockhamareto}, a single-shot GRPO framework for unit-test generation and selection, based on the principles of \emph{Ockham's Razor} and \emph{Pareto Optimality}. Ockhamareto has two principal components: (i)~a \emph{Pareto-gated Bonus} that rewards only rollouts non-dominated in~(mutation, $-$\#tests) space, and (ii)~\emph{Token-level Segment Credit}, which attributes each test's marginal mutation kills back to the tokens of its unit-test block. On the \emph{UnLeakedTestBench~(ULT)}, Ockhamareto \emph{strictly Pareto-dominates} the strongest RL baseline~(\emph{MIST-RL}). Furthermore, it dominates on {\em each and all} optimization objectives, catching more bugs ($49.9\%$ vs $31.3\%$ mutation score at $N{=}5$), using \emph{fewer} tests ($2.60$ vs $4.67$ on average), thereby achieving $3.4\times$ the per-test trade-off improvement. The advantage is found in all four benchmarks~(\emph{HumanEval+}, \emph{MBPP+}, \emph{CodeContests}, \emph{TestGenEval-Lite}): Ockhamareto leads both mutation and coverage metrics on every one, always with the smallest suite. Ockhamareto also outperforms the state-of-the-art at all model scales, adding $+30$--$35$~pp mutation at 4B, 9B, and 27B model sizes. We also show that the knee point of the optimal trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness on the Pareto front is not correlated with obvious more easily computed proxy metrics, such as function size. This finding motivates the Pareto front computation; it is needed to identify this crucial engineering trade-off for each function under test.

</details>


### [175] [LumiXAI: A Modular Full-Stack Framework for Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24524)
*Alfio Ferrara,Lorenzo Gatta,Sergio Picascia,Elisabetta Rocchetti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了LumiXAI，一个模块化的全栈可解释性分析框架，将分类和生成式归因分析整合到统一系统中，通过交互式GUI支持双向探索、插件架构和三级访问接口，为不同技能水平的用户提供可复现的归因分析服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的特征归因工具高度碎片化，各工具往往只专注于单一模态、单一API或GUI、固定的方法集，难以兼顾多方面需求。此外，多数可解释性工具主要为领域专家设计，要求编程技能或对归因方法的熟悉度，非专家用户难以使用。

Method: LumiXAI采用模块化全栈架构，核心包括：(1) 交互式GUI支持双向（模型与数据间）探索；(2) 插件架构支持注册新模型和新归因方法；(3) 三级访问层面向非程序员、开发者和扩展者，共享同一后端；(4) 容器化服务与持久化结果确保跨机器的可复现性。

Result: 论文提出了一个整合归因分析的系统框架，将分类归因与生成式归因统一在一个接口、一个交互模型和一个持久化层之下。

Conclusion: LumiXAI的贡献在于操作化了已有的归因方法，使其通过统一界面、统一交互模型和统一持久化层对各类用户可及，并通过容器化部署保证分析的可复现性。

Abstract: Feature attribution is a central tool of model interpretability, yet the software through which it is applied remains fragmented: individual tools specialize along narrow axes, such as a single modality, a code API or a GUI, or a fixed rather than extensible method set, and rarely combine these strengths. Moreover, many explainability tools are designed primarily for domain experts, requiring programming skills or familiarity with attribution methods that can make them difficult for non-expert users to access. In this article, we present LumiXAI, a modular full-stack framework that consolidates attribution analysis into a single system. It couples classification and generative attribution with an interactive GUI supporting bidirectional exploration, a plug-in architecture for registering new models and methods, and three access tiers serving non-programmers, developers, and extenders from one backend. Its contribution is a system that operationalises established attribution methods under one interface, one interaction model, and one persistence layer, with containerised services and persistent results making analyses reproducible across machines.

</details>


### [176] [Quantifying the Relationship Between Team Dysfunctions and Performance in Capstone Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24634)
*Luciano Pereira Soares,Luciana Campos Lima,Rafael Corsi Ferrão*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了工程Capstone项目中团队功能失调与项目绩效之间的关系，利用Lencioni的五功能失调模型量化团队动态，发现团队动态与绩效之间存在弱到中等的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Capstone项目被认为是评估工程和计算机科学学生专业实践准备程度的关键环节，但团队动态与项目绩效之间的关系尚未被充分理解，因此需要实证研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 收集学生团队的调查数据，基于Lencioni的五功能失调团队模型量化团队动态，所有指标采用反向编码（数值越高表示更健康的团队行为），每个功能失调分数为三个对应调查项之和，范围从3到9。

Result: 团队动态与绩效之间存在弱到中等的相关性。研究发现目标一致性和对可交付成果的关注尤为重要。

Conclusion: 团队动态应与其他技术和情境因素一同考量，研究为Capstone项目的设计提供了洞见，强调了团队动态在项目式学习环境中作用的重要性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the association between team dysfunctions and performance in the context of an engineering Capstone program. Capstone programs are considered fundamental in assessing the readiness of engineering and computer science students for professional practice, evaluating not only technical knowledge but also essential competencies such as teamwork, communication, design, and organization. Despite the recognized importance of team dynamics in such settings, their relationship with project performance remains insufficiently understood. Using survey data collected from student teams, we quantify team dynamics based on Lencioni's model of the five dysfunctions of a team, with all measures reverse-coded so that higher values indicate more positive team behaviors (i.e., lower dysfunction). The results indicate a weak-to-moderate relationship between team dynamics and performance. Each dysfunction score was computed as the sum of three corresponding survey items. All items were coded such that higher values indicate more positive team behaviors (i.e., lower dysfunction). Therefore, each score ranges from 3 to 9, where higher values reflect healthier team dynamics. This study analyzes empirical evidence on team effectiveness in project-based learning environments and offers insights for the design of Capstone programs. In particular, it highlights the importance of goal alignment and focus on deliverables while emphasizing that team dynamics should be considered alongside technical and contextual factors.

</details>


### [177] [Aging of Prompt Engineering Techniques Across LLM Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24641)
*Anastasiia Rudyk,Julian Oertel,Regina Hebig*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对Khojah等人（2025）的研究进行了部分复制，评估五种提示工程技术在三个模型版本对（GPT-3.5/GPT-4o、Qwen2/Qwen2.5、Mistral-7B/Mistral-Large）上的函数级代码生成效果，发现提示工程效果随模型家族和代际呈现特异性的"老化"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型频繁更新，但提示工程技术在跨代模型上的有效性变化尚不明确，现有提示工程实践可能无法直接迁移到新模型。

Method: 对五种提示工程技术（Zero-Shot、Few-Shot、CoT、CCoT和改编版PoT）在六个指令微调模型（三个版本对）上进行评估，使用CodePromptEval数据集的218个Python函数子集，通过pass@k功能正确性指标分析19,620次生成。

Result: 提示工程以模型家族特异性的方式"老化"：GPT模型的结构化提示边际收益递减甚至为负（提示技能被内化）；Qwen模型仍从Few-Shot和CCoT中获得显著收益；Mistral模型表现混合，CCoT收益持续但CoT和PoT收益减弱。

Conclusion: 有效的提示策略必须根据模型家族和代际进行调整，而非直接迁移，这推动了自适应、模型感知提示和更广泛的多维代码质量评估的未来研究。

Abstract: Prompt engineering and prompt engineering techniques (PETs) have become an integral part of software engineering for AI systems. However, new LLMs are released frequently and it remains unclear how the effectiveness of prompt engineering techniques changes across successive generations of Large Language Models (LLMs). To this end, we conduct a partial replication of the study by Khojah et al. (2025). We evaluate five techniques - Zero-Shot, Few-Shot, Chain-of-Thought (CoT), Contrastive Chain-of-Thought (CCoT), and an adapted version of Program-of-Thought (PoT) - on six instruction-tuned models grouped into three version pairs: GPT-3.5-Turbo/GPT-4o, Qwen2 7B Instruct/Qwen2.5 7B Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct/Mistral-Large. We use a cleaned subset of the CodePromptEval dataset with 218 context-rich Python functions and 19,620 total generations assessed via pass@k-based functional correctness to evaluate model pairs on function-level code generation tasks. We show that prompt engineering "ages" in a model-family-specific way: Newer GPT models exhibit diminishing or even negative marginal gains from structured prompting, suggesting that instruction-following and reasoning scaffolds are increasingly internalized, whereas Qwen models continue to benefit substantially from Few-Shot and CCoT. Mistral models show mixed behavior with persistent gains from CCoT but attenuated benefits from CoT and PoT. Our results imply that effective prompting strategies must be adapted per model family and generation rather than transferred unchanged. This motivates future work on adaptive, model-aware prompting and broader, multi-dimensional code quality evaluation.

</details>


### [178] [A Literate Programming Environment for Human and Machine Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24644)
*Adam T. Burke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于构建文学编程（literate programming）环境的系统，该环境与语言感知的机器智能体协同工作，通过将代码、自然语言和结构化数据共置在可执行程序散文中，优化LLM上下文窗口的使用，并为LLM编码智能体提供更接近人类IDE的符号感知搜索工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的编程环境主要面向人类程序员，而LLM编码智能体缺乏类似IDE中符号感知搜索和用法信息的工具。此外，LLM的上下文窗口有限，需要更高效地将代码与其相关自然语言和结构化数据上下文集成为一体。

Method: 核心方法包括四个组件：1）可执行程序散文的语法规则；2）将名称视为一等对象的解析器；3）连接散文、名称与可执行产物的内部名称图；4）与现有编程语言和测试工具集成的绑定机制。

Result: 作者描述了一个可工作的实现，支持三种成熟的编程语言绑定，并提供多个示例程序。该环境能够实现代码与自然语言及结构化数据上下文的共同定位，并赋予LLM编码智能体更强大的工具集。

Conclusion: 通过将文学编程理念与现代LLM智能体相结合，该环境有望提升LLM编码智能体理解和使用代码的能力，使其更接近人类程序员在IDE中的开发体验，从而更有效地利用上下文窗口和符号信息。

Abstract: This paper introduces an environment for constructing literate programs in concert with language-aware machine agents. This environment includes a grammar for executable program essays, a parser that treats names as first-class objects, an internal name-graph which relates prose, names and executable artifacts, and a binding mechanism for existing languages and testing toolsets. This supports co-location of code with its most relevant natural language and structured data context, making better use of Large Language Model (LLM) context windows. It also provides LLM coding agents with a toolset more analogous to the symbol-aware search and usage information available in human programmer-facing Integrated Development Environments (IDEs). We describe a working implementation with bindings to three established programming languages, and several example programs.

</details>


### [179] [Causal Explanations of Process Monitor Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24672)
*Tom Yaacov,Nathan Blake,Hana Chockler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于实际因果框架的过程监控预测局部解释方法，通过构建捕捉事件间时间依赖的因果模型来计算事件责任度，生成稳定且简洁的案例级解释，并在标准PPM基准数据集上验证了其优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测过程监控（PPM）中的AI模型虽然预测性能高，但黑盒特性使得难以理解预测背后的原因，需要一种能够生成局部（案例级）解释的方法来揭示事件对预测结果的影响。

Method: 基于实际因果框架，定义适合过程的因果模型，捕捉轨迹中事件间的时间依赖关系；利用该模型隐式计算因果原因并量化不同事件对预测结果的重要性；提出模型无关的算法来近似事件责任度。

Result: 在包含多达130,000条轨迹、最长1,800个事件和400种事件类型的标准PPM基准数据集上评估，与现有局部解释方法相比，本方法生成更稳定、更简洁的解释，同时保持具有竞争力的效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的实际因果解释方法能够为过程监控预测提供可靠且可理解的案例级解释，在稳定性和简洁性上优于现有方法，为该领域提供了新的解释性分析工具。

Abstract: Process mining is widely used to diagnose processes and identify performance and compliance issues. Specifically, Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) techniques use AI models to predict outcomes of ongoing process instances. While these models can achieve high predictive performance, their black-box nature makes it difficult to understand the underlying reasons behind their output predictions. In this paper, we propose a novel approach for generating local (case-level) explanations of process monitor predictions based on the framework of actual causality. We define a causal model tailored to processes that captures temporal dependencies between events in a trace, thus allowing us to reason about causal influence of events on the predicted outcome. Our method uses this model implicitly to compute causes and quantify the importance of different events with respect to the predicted outcome. We present a practical, model-agnostic algorithm that approximates event responsibility given the process structure reflected in the causal model. We evaluate our approach on a range of datasets derived from real-life event logs from a standard PPM benchmark. Each dataset contains up to 130,000 traces, with trace lengths of up to 1,800 events and up to 400 distinct event types. We compare our approach with state-of-the-art local explanation methods. The results demonstrate that our approach produces more stable and concise explanations while maintaining competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [180] [''You Can't Open an LLM With a Screwdriver'': The De-Democratization of Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24720)
*Zixuan Feng,Italo Santos,Kostadin Damevski,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇观点论文，反驳了"生成式AI将终结编程"的预测，指出AI虽然能拓宽代码生产的"访问"能力，但软件的"控制"权（检查、评估、集成、维护与治理）可能更加集中。软件工程专业知识的重心正从编码转向意图规范、AI行为编排与治理、软件行为评估及系统集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对"生成式AI即将取代人类编程"的流行预测，作者认为这种假设过于简化，忽视了代码访问与控制之间的区别——AI让更多人能生成代码，但不等于所有人都能掌控软件的可靠性与可维护性，需要重新审视AI对软件工程专业知识的真实影响。

Method: 本文采用专家小组（expert panel）讨论为基础，结合观点论证的方式阐述核心论点，并在此基础上提出面向教育、工具和政策的未来研究方向。

Result: 论文提出两个核心洞见：（1）AI扩大代码生产的访问权，却可能加剧控制权的集中；（2）软件工程专业知识的重心正向意图规范转移，即编排和治理AI行为、评估软件行为和集成软件系统。

Conclusion: AI并不会消除软件工程专业知识，而是改变其关键所在。论文呼吁软件工程社区通过教育、工具和政策方面的研究，在AI时代获得更大的自主性、问责性和适应性。

Abstract: Claims that generative AI will soon write all of the code have led to predictions that programming is nearing its end. In this vision paper, we argue against this assumption that broader access to code generation necessarily democratizes software development, i.e., everyone can code but we have to distinguish between access and control: by access, we mean the ability of more people, including non-experts and less-experienced developers, to generate code-like artifacts with AI; by control, we mean the capacity to inspect, evaluate, integrate, maintain, and govern those artifacts as dependable software. While AI may broaden access to code production, control may become more concentrated among those who own or understand the code, software practices, infrastructure, evaluation practices, and deployment pipelines.
  Grounded in an expert panel, our vision paper argues that AI does not eliminate software engineering expertise but shifts where that expertise becomes most critical. The locus of software engineering expertise is shifting toward intent specification: orchestrating and governing AI behavior, evaluating software behavior, and integrating software systems. We conclude this paper by identifying research opportunities for education, tools, and policy that can help the software engineering community respond to the AI era with greater agency, accountability, and adaptability.

</details>


### [181] [From Natural Language Requirements to Graphical User Interfaces: Automated Prototyping and Verification with Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24749)
*Kristian Kolthoff*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对GUI原型设计和需求验证两大挑战，提出了基于NL的GUI检索与重排序方法、LLM适配GUI生成技术，以及基于LLM和多模态智能体的需求验证方法，显著减少了人工开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求获取对交互式软件系统开发至关重要，但自然语言的歧义性容易导致误解。形式化规格需要专业知识，而高保真GUI原型制作耗时费力；需求验证仍以手动为主，现有自动化方法局限于静态规则。

Method: 针对C1（减少从自然语言需求生成GUI原型的工作量），提出了基于NL的GUI检索与重排序方法、新基准数据集、高效适配LLM进行GUI生成的技术（包括专有GUI表示）。针对C2（减少GUI应用和原型的需求验证工作量），提出了基于LLM的方法验证静态GUI原型上的语义复杂自然语言需求，并引入多模态LLM代理通过自动生成和评估的交互轨迹来验证动态GUI应用中的复杂功能和需求。

Result: 所提出的方法在大规模带人工标注的基准上展示了有效性，显著减少了GUI原型制作和需求验证中的手动工作量。

Conclusion: 本文的方法有效解决了GUI原型制作和需求验证中的两大挑战，通过LLM技术大幅降低人工成本，提升了需求工程流程的效率。

Abstract: Requirements elicitation is essential for developing interactive software systems, as it helps ensure that the resulting product meets stakeholder needs. Since elicitation typically relies on natural language (NL), misunderstandings can arise from its inherent ambiguity. Formal specifications can reduce ambiguity but require technical expertise. GUI prototyping therefore provides a valuable alternative by turning requirements into tangible visual artifacts that support communication, elicitation, and validation. However, creating high-fidelity prototypes remains time-consuming and costly. Similarly, requirements verification, which ensures that implementations conform to specified requirements, is still largely manual, while existing automated approaches are often limited to static, rule-based techniques. This work addresses two challenges: (C1) reducing the effort required to transform NL requirements into GUI prototypes, and (C2) reducing the effort required for requirements verification in GUI applications and prototypes. For C1, we introduce novel NL-based GUI retrieval and reranking methods, new benchmarks, and techniques for efficiently adapting LLMs to GUI generation, including proprietary GUI representations. Their effectiveness is demonstrated on a large benchmark with human annotations. For C2, we propose LLM-based methods for verifying semantically complex NL requirements on static GUI prototypes and introduce a multimodal LLM-based agent for verifying complex functional and non-functional requirements in dynamic GUI applications through automatically generated and evaluated interaction trajectories. Overall, the proposed methods substantially reduce manual effort in GUI prototyping and requirements verification.

</details>


### [182] [Automatic Model Card Generation Using an LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24807)
*Tajkia Rahman Toma,Balreet Grewal,Cor-Paul Bezemer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个基于LLM的工具：MCTidy用于将现有模型卡片重组为标准模板，MCGenie用于直接从模型仓库数据生成模型卡片。在48个Hugging Face模型卡片上评估，两者均表现出高信息保留率、高语义相似度和良好的事实正确性，证明了LLM方法可实现可扩展、标准化的模型文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型卡片作为机器学习模型透明性和可问责性的关键文档，但缺乏一致的结构，且许多模型没有提供模型卡片，导致模型间的比较和解读变得困难。

Method: 提出两个LLM-based系统：1）MCTidy，将现有模型卡片重组为标准模板，从信息保留、章节对齐、幻觉和稳定性四个维度评估；2）MCGenie，从模型仓库数据直接生成模型卡片，评估语义相似度、事实正确性和对输入资源的敏感性。

Result: MCTidy在48个Hugging Face模型卡片上展示了高信息保留率和准确的章节分配，幻觉罕见且主要出现在描述性部分，运行稳定性强。MCGenie生成的模型卡片语义相似度均值约0.9，超过半数完全正确，其余错误多为轻微问题；生成质量强烈依赖配套资源（尤其是相关论文）的可用性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明LLM-based方法在实现可扩展、标准化的模型卡片文档方面具有很大潜力，能够为提升机器学习模型的透明度与可比较性提供有效支持。

Abstract: Model cards are structured documents that summarize key information about machine learning models to improve transparency, usability, and accountability. However, they often lack a consistent structure, and many models provide no model cards, making comparison and interpretation difficult. This paper presents two contributions. First, we propose MCTidy, an LLM-based approach that reorganizes existing model cards into a standardized template to improve clarity and comparability. Second, we introduce MCGenie, an LLM-based system that generates model cards directly from model repository data. We apply MCTidy to 48 Hugging Face model cards and evaluate information retention, section alignment, hallucination, and stability. Our findings show high information retention with minimal textual loss, accurate section assignment, rare hallucinations primarily in descriptive sections, and strong stability across runs. We assess MCGenie by generating model cards for the same 48 models and assessing semantic similarity, factual correctness, and sensitivity to input resources. The generated model cards achieved high semantic similarity (mean around 0.9); over half were fully correct, and most remaining errors were minor. Generation quality depended strongly on the availability of supporting resources, particularly associated papers. Overall, our findings demonstrate the potential of LLM-based methods to enable scalable, standardized model card documentation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Effective Pivot Attack Detection via System and Network Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23731)
*Ava Powelson,Carson Kuzniar,Hyojoon Kim,Israat Haque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Stitch，一种基于主机的系统，利用可编程内核实时检测网络中的pivoting攻击。通过进程追踪结合系统与网络层信息，连接进出通信并识别pivoting特征，相比现有最先进方案在准确率上提升31%，误报率最大仅0.006%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于边界的安全设备（如防火墙和入侵检测系统）无法有效防御利用pivoting技术的现代攻击。由于中继流量的合法性外观，pivoting极难被检测，而现有防御方案存在处理延迟高、准确率低或依赖全网络协同等缺陷。

Method: Stitch作为主机级系统，利用可编程内核实时检测pivoting。通过观察穿越主机的流量，使用进程追踪技术有效结合系统和网络层面的信息，将进出通信进行关联，识别二者之间的pivoting特征。

Result: 与最先进的pivoting防御方案相比，Stitch在准确率上获得了31%的提升，在两次真实世界部署中最大误报率仅为0.006%。

Conclusion: Stitch填补了当前pivoting检测方案中的空白，为网络中的易受攻击主机提供了准确、轻量且独立的安全覆盖。

Abstract: Perimeter-based security appliances, such as firewalls or Intrusion Detection Systems, are ineffective against modern attacks that use pivoting, wherein attackers "pivot" traffic through compromised hosts to gain access to additional targets that would otherwise be inaccessible. Due to the legitimate appearance of the relayed traffic, pivoting is extremely difficult to detect. Although the consequences of these attacks are known to be severe, existing defenses suffer from drawbacks such as high processing delays, low accuracy, or reliance on network-wide participation, making them inconvenient or even ineffective. This work presents Stitch, a host-based system that uses the programmable kernel to detect pivoting in real time. By observing host-traversing flows, Stitch uses process tracing to effectively combine system and network-level information, connecting incoming and outgoing communications and identifying pivoting characteristics between them. Showing 31% gains in accuracy over state-of-the-art pivoting defenses and a maximum false positive rate of 0.006% over two separate real-world deployments, Stitch covers the gap in current pivot detection solutions by providing accurate, lightweight, and independent coverage for vulnerable hosts in a network.

</details>


### [184] [TrustShiftProbe: Characterizing, Benchmarking, and Defending Staged Trust Attacks on MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23763)
*Mehrdad Rostamzadeh,Sidhant Narula,Mohammad Ghasemigol,Daniel Takabi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustShift攻击：MCP服务器在初始条件化阶段表现良性以建立信任，达到交互阈值后切换到恶意负载，实现时间维度的规避攻击。作者提出了TrustShiftProbe评估框架和SHIELD运行时防御机制，在多种前沿模型上将攻击成功率从69.5%降至42.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP已成为LLM智能体连接外部工具的标准协议层，但服务器的开放性引入了严重的安全威胁。现有静态分析只能看到诚实阶段，无法检测服务器的延迟背叛行为，因此需要新的威胁模型和防御方案。

Method: 提出了TrustShiftProbe框架，包含四个部分：(1) 状态化时间威胁模型（良性条件化阶段+信任时界处的对抗性背叛）；(2) 语言无关的攻击引擎，在四个生产领域实例化攻击；(3) SHIELD多层级零预言机运行时防御，在MCP传输边界审计服务器负载；(4) 九种TrustShift变体的分类体系（覆盖三种执行机制×三种对抗目标）。

Result: 在多个前沿专有和开源权重模型上，TrustShift攻击达到69.5%的平均攻击成功率，SHIELD将其缓解至42.7%。

Conclusion: TrustShift揭示了MCP生态中服务器端的时间维规避威胁，SHIELD证明了在传输边界进行行为基线审计可以有效缓解此类攻击，但仍需进一步改进以更低误报率实现更强的防御。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has emerged as the standard layer connecting Large Language Model agents to external tool backends. This openness introduces a severe server-side threat we term TrustShift: a compromised MCP server behaves benignly during an initial conditioning phase, building operational reliance and suppressing agent skepticism, before switching to an adversarial payload once an interaction threshold is reached. The evasion is temporal, not syntactic: benign at deploy time, the server's defection is invisible to predeployment static analysis, which sees only the honest phase. Switched payloads range from overt structural violations to schema-valid manipulations, the latter preserving outer protocol compliance to evade runtime middleware filters. Crucially, TrustShift originates in the server-controlled tool channel, not user prompts (unlike indirect prompt injection) or the transport (unlike man-in-the-middle): the adversary is the trusted server endpoint itself. We introduce TrustShiftProbe, an evaluation and defense framework with four contributions: (1) a stateful temporal threat model of the agent-server lifecycle as a benign conditioning phase followed by an adversarial defection at a trust horizon; (2) a language-agnostic attack engine that instantiates each variant as a compromised MCP server across four production domains; (3) SHIELD, a multi-tier, zero-oracle runtime defense at the MCP transport boundary that audits server payloads against behavioral baselines learned during clean trust windows; and (4) a taxonomy of nine TrustShift variants spanning three execution mechanisms (structural violation, semantic corruption, scope expansion) and three adversarial objectives (disruption, exfiltration, and their combination). Across frontier proprietary and open-weight models, TrustShift attacks achieve a 69.5% mean attack success rate that SHIELD mitigates to 42.7%.

</details>


### [185] [ROBBIN: Rowhammer-Based Backdoor Injection during Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23774)
*Saion K. Roy,Yufei Wang,A. Adam Ding,Yunsi Fei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ROBBIN，一种硬件感知的Rowhammer后门注入攻击方法，将DRAM设备的特定位翻转模式纳入后门构建过程，通过迭代选择模型权重的page映射来最大化攻击成功率并保持测试准确率，在多种DRAM设备上实现了稳健且可靠的后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Rowhammer推理时后门攻击仅从算法层面设计位翻转策略，忽略了底层DRAM硬件实际产生的位翻转行为，导致算法后门构建与硬件实现脱节。这种不匹配会造成非预期位置的附带位翻转，降低攻击成功率和正常输入的测试准确率，且攻击性能在不同DRAM设备间差异显著。

Method: ROBBIN首先表征目标DRAM的位翻转模式，利用该信息迭代地为模型权重选择DRAM page映射，在Rowhammer操作下最大化ASR同时保持TA。该方法将每次锤击诱导的位翻转视为攻击设计的组成部分，而非事后忽略附带翻转。

Result: 在ResNet-20和VGG-16配合CIFAR-10数据集、三种商用DDR4芯片上的评估中，ROBBIN持续达到接近90%的攻击成功率，同时保持高于83%的测试准确率，展示了跨不同DRAM设备的可靠后门有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将设备特定的硬件漏洞特性整合进后门构建过程，ROBBIN解决了硬件无感知后门策略在不同DRAM设备上表现不一致的问题，实现了跨设备稳健的后门攻击。

Abstract: Existing Rowhammer-based inference-time backdoor attacks design their bit-flip strategies purely at the algorithmic level, without accounting for the bit-flips that the underlying DRAM hardware will actually produce. This disconnection between the algorithmic backdoor construction and its hardware realization leads to unreliable attack performance, as collateral bit-flips at unintended locations degrade both the attack success rate (ASR) on triggered inputs and the test accuracy (TA) for normal inputs. Consequently, the performance of such attacks varies significantly across different DRAM devices, as each device presents a unique set of exploitable bit-flip locations. This work presents ROBBIN, a hardware-aware Rowhammer-based backdoor injection attack that integrates the device-specific vulnerability into the backdoor construction process. ROBBIN first characterizes the bit-flip patterns of a target DRAM and uses this information to iteratively select DRAM \textit{page} mappings for the model weights that would maximize ASR while preserving TA under Rowhammering. By treating every hammering-induced bit-flip as an integral part of the attack design rather than first constructing a hardware-agnostic backdoor and dismissing collateral flips as side effects, ROBBIN produces backdoors that remain robust across devices. Evaluated on ResNet-20 and VGG-16 with CIFAR-10 across three commodity DDR4 chips, ROBBIN consistently achieves close to 90\% ASR while maintaining TA above 83\%, demonstrating reliable backdoor efficacy across diverse DRAM devices.

</details>


### [186] [A Scenario-Based Evaluation of CRQC+AI Vulnerability Spectrum for TLS 1.3 Cryptographic Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23785)
*Noel Grover,Mussie Haile,Brad Pedersen,Eric Uner,Bradley J Erickson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了量子计算和AI加速对TLS 1.3密码学依赖的风险，采用基于证据分层的模型，区分机制支持型威胁（Shor算法对RSA和ECC）、应急支持型风险（格基PQC）和假设型风险（哈希与对称原语）。通过四情景能力模型和参数敏感性分析，模型预测RSA风险在2030-2032年间突破50%阈值，PQC风险在2032-2035年后变为非零（在维度坍缩假设成立条件下）。截至2026年中，未发现任何NIST批准算法被攻破，但论文敦促按照2030/2031联邦期限强制PQC迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子威胁评估缺乏可复现、参数可更新的方法论工具。论文旨在提供一个证据分层、情景驱动的可复现风险评估框架，弥补量子资源估算研究与专家启发式时间线调查之间的方法论缺口。

Method: 采用证据分层模型区分三类风险（机制支持型、应急支持型、假设型）；构建四情景能力模型，包含显式参数和伪代码以确保可复现性；执行参数敏感性分析和显式证伪分析；制定参数更新协议，将观测到的符合/偏离与模型曲线映射到具体参数修订上。

Result: 截至2026年中，NIST批准的算法均未被攻破；RSA风险在2030-2032年间跨过50%阈值；PQC风险在2032-2035年后的应急情景下变为非零（条件依赖于未证实的维度坍缩假设）；未发现ML-KEM、ML-DSA、SLH-DSA或AES-256的已知破解。

Conclusion: 论文建议PQC迁移应作为2030/2031联邦期限的强制要求（基于Mosca HNDL推理），同时强调密码敏捷性和混合密码部署是PQC迁移的必要补充。

Abstract: This paper evaluates quantum and AI-accelerated risks to TLS 1.3 cryptographic dependencies under an evidence-tiered model, distinguishing mechanism-backed threats (Shor algorithm against RSA and ECC) from contingency-backed risks to lattice-based post-quantum cryptography (PQC) and hypothesis-only risks to hash-based and symmetric primitives. We do not identify any known breaks of ML-KEM, ML-DSA, SLH-DSA, or AES-256. Instead, we use explicit scenario assumptions, organized as a four-scenario capability model with parameters and pseudocode for reproducibility, to stress-test migration timelines accompanied by parameter sensitivity analysis and explicit falsification analysis. The primary methodological contribution is a reproducible scenario-estimation instrument together with its explicit update mechanics: every parameter is a named, anchored quantity that can be varied and the model rerun; a stated protocol maps observed conformance to, or deviation from, the modeled curves onto revisions of specific parameters, so progressive refinements can be tested against accumulating historical data. The paper is a methodological companion to quantum resource-estimation studies and to expert-elicitation timeline surveys such as the Global Risk Institute quantum threat reports, with its revision rules stated explicitly. As of mid-2026, the model does not show any NIST-approved algorithms as broken. Instead, the vulnerability spectrum under different scenarios shows RSA risk crossing the 50% threshold between 2030-2032 and the PQC risk becoming a non-zero risk after 2032-2035 under contingency scenarios conditional on the unproven dimension-collapse. We urge PQC migration as mandatory per the 2030 and 2031 federal deadlines and by Mosca HNDL reasoning, and that crypto-agility and hybrid cryptographic deployment be considered necessary complements to any PQC migration efforts.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond the Mandate: A Systematic Security Analysis of the Agent Payments Protocol (AP2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23858)
*Avital Aviv,Parth A. Gandh,Ron Bitton,Asaf Shabtai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Google提出的Agent支付协议（AP2）v0.2进行了系统性的安全分析，识别出48个威胁并利用AIVSS评分，发现8个高危威胁，并构建了覆盖所有部署架构的测试平台和概念验证演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AP2 v0.1已被发现存在重放和提示注入攻击，v0.2虽修复了部分问题但新增了功能和部署假设，需要重新进行安全分析以评估其安全性。

Method: 基于角色、交易生命周期、部署架构和信任边界进行系统性分析，将生命周期划分为五个阶段并识别五种部署架构；使用MAESTRO框架建模四个威胁行为者、十一个攻击面、十八个对手能力和六个攻击者目标；使用AIVSS对威胁进行评分；构建涵盖所有架构的测试平台并进行概念验证演示。

Result: 生成包含48个威胁的目录，覆盖五个攻击家族；识别出八个在至少一种架构中达到高危等级的威胁；开发了五个覆盖所有高危威胁的概念验证演示及其缓解措施；开发了部署感知扫描器。

Conclusion: 有效的托管签名本身并不能确保代理中介交易反映用户意图，当交易授权前的上下文被操纵时，仍需关注授权前阶段的完整性问题。

Abstract: The Agent Payments Protocol (AP2), introduced by Google, enables large language model (LLM)-driven shopping agents to authorize and execute payments on behalf of users. Its signed Checkout and Payment Mandates protect the integrity of transaction data after signing. Agent interactions and external inputs that shape a transaction before authorization remain outside that protection, including Agent-to-Agent Protocol (A2A) messages and Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool calls. Prior work identified replay and prompt-injection attacks in AP2 v0.1. AP2 v0.2 addresses some of these issues but adds capabilities and deployment assumptions that require renewed analysis. We present a systematic security analysis of AP2 v0.2 based on its roles, transaction lifecycle, deployment architectures, and trust boundaries. We divide the lifecycle into five phases and identify five deployment architectures. Using MAESTRO (Multi-Agent Environment, Security, Threat, Risk, Outcome), we model four threat actors, eleven attack surfaces, eighteen adversary capabilities, and six attacker goals. The resulting catalog contains 48 threats spanning five attack families. We score these threats with the Artificial Intelligence Vulnerability Scoring System (AIVSS), identifying eight that reach the High band in at least one architecture. Because no complete public AP2 deployment was available, we build a testbed spanning all five architectures and develop five proof-of-concept demonstrations covering all eight High-risk threats and their mitigations. We also develop a deployment-aware scanner that maps applicable threats to static, cross-role consistency, and adversarial checks. Our analysis shows that valid mandate signatures alone do not ensure that an agent-mediated transaction reflects the user's intent when its pre-authorization context is manipulated.

</details>


### [188] [SoK: ARCUS: On the Efficiency and Efficacy of Hardware Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23933)
*Alenkruth Krishnan Murali,Raghul Saravanan,Sai Manoj P D,Ashish Venkat*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对现代硬件模糊测试技术进行了全面分析，覆盖ISA、微架构和RTL三个抽象层，权衡了输入刺激质量、变异策略、反馈机制等因素，识别了当前实践的不足并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件模糊测试在不同抽象层面面临不同挑战，但目前缺乏对各抽象层硬件模糊测试技术的系统性和比较性分析。作者旨在填补这一空白，帮助研究者理解各层的设计权衡与未满足的需求。

Method: 对三个主要抽象层（ISA、微架构、RTL）的硬件模糊测试技术进行综合分析，考察多个关键因素，包括输入刺激质量、变异策略、反馈机制、目标平台、参考模型和覆盖率，并对比分析各层的挑战、目标与设计权衡。

Result: 研究发现各抽象层的挑战、目标和设计权衡差异显著；识别出当前硬件模糊测试实践中的多个未满足需求，包括智能输入生成、可靠可扩展的金标准参考模型、表达力强的反馈通道以及跨层集成。

Conclusion: 基于研究洞察，提出了未来研究方向：混合模糊测试框架、AI辅助测试生成、可扩展参考模型、标准化评估指标与基准、以及人机协同自动化引导探索与分析，旨在实现高效、可靠且全面的硬件验证解决方案。

Abstract: This work presents a comprehensive analysis of contemporary hardware fuzzing techniques applied across three major abstraction layers: Instruction Set Architecture (ISA), microarchitecture, and Register-Transfer Level (RTL). Our study examines key factors including input stimulus quality, mutation strategies, feedback mechanisms, target platforms, reference models, and achieved coverage. We find challenges, goals, and design trade-offs vary significantly across abstraction layers. We further identify several unmet needs in current hardware fuzzing practices, such as intelligent input generation, reliable and scalable golden reference models, expressive feedback channels, and cross-layer integration. Building on these insights, we outline future research directions, including hybrid fuzzing frameworks, AI-assisted test generation, scalable reference models, standardized evaluation metrics and benchmarks, and human-in-the-loop automation for guided exploration and analysis. Together, they aim to unlock efficient, reliable, and comprehensive hardware verification solutions.

</details>


### [189] [STAIN-FL: Stealthy Targeted Attack Injection with Contextual Triggers in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23952)
*Ashlinder Kaur,Purnima Murali Mohan,Zengxiang Li,Tram Truong-Huu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出STAIN-FL，一种面向联邦视频异常检测的隐蔽后门攻击注入框架，利用自然监控场景（低光、室内、人群密度）作为上下文触发器，通过标签操纵和梯度掩蔽实现低可检测性、高持续性的稀疏攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦视频异常检测在无需共享原始监控画面的情况下协作训练模型，但服务器侧可见性有限，使恶意客户端能够通过恶意更新注入后门。现有研究缺乏利用监控场景自然条件作为触发器的隐蔽攻击方案。

Method: STAIN-FL框架结合两种关键技术：一是异常到正常的标签操纵（anomaly-to-benign label manipulation），利用自然监控条件（低光场景、室内环境、人群密度）作为上下文触发器；二是对最小更新坐标进行梯度掩蔽（gradient masking over least-updated coordinates），以保持干净精度同时诱导触发条件相关的错误分类。

Result: 在UCF-Crime数据集上使用1024维I3D特征，在非IID四客户端多机构设置下，对比FedAvg和FedProx。稀疏攻击保持平均干净精度下降低于2%，但在FedAvg下后门准确率峰值达56.7%，FedProx下达54.2%。在FedAvg下，稀疏后门在732个攻击后轮次中平均有336轮保持在25%后门准确率阈值以上。

Conclusion: STAIN-FL证明了上下文触发的稀疏后门攻击在联邦视频异常检测中具有低可检测性和高持久性风险，这凸显了监控系统中上下文触发攻击的严重安全隐患，也对现有联邦防御机制提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Federated video anomaly detection trains model collaboratively without sharing raw surveillance footage, but limited server-side visibility lets compromised clients to inject backdoor via malicious updates. This paper introduces STAIN-FL, a stealthy targeted backdoor attack injection framework that uses naturally occurring surveillance conditions, including low-light scenes, indoor settings, and crowd density, as contextual triggers. STAIN-FL combines anomaly-to-benign label \textit{manipulation} with gradient masking over least-updated coordinates to preserve clean accuracy while inducing trigger-conditioned misclassification. We evaluate STAIN-FL on \texttt{UCF-Crime} using 1024-dimensional I3D features in a non-IID four-client multi-agency setting, comparing FedAvg and FedProx under sparse and continuous attacks. Results show that sparse attacks have low-detectability, operationally significant attacks rather than high-intensity attacks: they keep the mean clean-accuracy drop below $2\%$, yet still misclassify more than half of triggered anomalies at peak backdoor accuracy under FedAvg ($56.7\%$) and FedProx ($54.2\%$). Under FedAvg, the sparse backdoor remains above the $25\%$ backdoor-accuracy threshold for an average of $336$ post-attack rounds, highlighting the persistence risk of contextually triggered attacks in surveillance systems.

</details>


### [190] [NeuronGuard: Robust LLM Safety Alignment via Ablation-Aware Safety Signal Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23959)
*Anjun Gao,Yueyang Quan,Yufei Xia,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了NeuronGuard，一种在微调阶段同时防御越狱攻击和神经元级攻击的方法，通过将安全信号重新分布到更广泛的神经元上，实现近零攻击成功率且保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的安全对齐在面对不断增长的攻击手段时仍然脆弱：越狱攻击通过精心构造的提示词绕过安全机制，而神经元级攻击则在部署后直接剪除安全关键神经元。两者的共同弱点是安全相关信息集中在稀疏的神经元子集中。

Method: NeuronGuard包含三个核心组件：(1)通过定期刷新的逐层线性分类器动态识别安全关键神经元；(2)在故意神经元消融下强制拒绝行为；(3)应用KL散度正则化保证分布一致性。同时采用随机梯度投影策略解决防御与任务目标之间的冲突，保持下游任务效用。

Result: 在三个LLM、六种最新攻击策略以及多模态设置下的实验表明，NeuronGuard实现了近零攻击成功率，同时保持任务准确率，包括对白盒自适应攻击者的防御。还提供了形式化保证，证明其严格降低了攻击成功率上界。

Conclusion: NeuronGuard通过将安全信号重新分布到更广泛的神经元集合中，同时强化了对越狱攻击和神经元级剪除攻击的防御能力，提供了一种在微调阶段实现鲁棒安全对齐的有效方案。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) remains brittle against a growing spectrum of attacks. Jailbreak attacks bypass safety mechanisms through crafted prompts, while neuron-level attacks directly prune safety-critical neurons post-deployment. Both exploit a common weakness: safety-relevant information concentrates in a sparse neuron subset. We present NeuronGuard, a fine-tuning-stage defense that simultaneously hardens LLMs against both attack classes by redistributing safety signals across a broader set of neurons. NeuronGuard dynamically identifies safety-critical neurons via periodically refreshed per-layer linear classifiers, forces refusal behavior under deliberate neuron ablation, and applies KL-divergence regularization for distributional consistency. A randomized gradient projection strategy preserves downstream task utility by resolving conflicts between the defense and task objectives. We provide a formal guarantee that NeuronGuard strictly reduces the attack success rate (ASR) upper bound, and experiments across three LLMs, six state-of-the-art attack strategies, and multimodal settings confirm near-zero ASR while maintaining task accuracy, including against white-box adaptive adversaries.

</details>


### [191] [RAGSentinel: Certifiable Geometric Consensus for Robust Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23965)
*Yueyang Quan,Anjun Gao,Yufei Xia,Minghong Fang,Zhuqing Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RAGSentinel，一种无需训练、无需标签的黑盒RAG系统防御方法，通过代理编码器测量查询条件化的隐藏状态偏移，去除共享主题方向，并将中毒文档作为几何异常值从稳健多数共识中过滤掉。实验证明其在多个数据集、LLM家族和攻击场景下均能有效降低攻击成功率并保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通过外部文档提升大模型事实性，但恶意文档注入知识库后可操控模型输出错误答案。现有事后检索防御（基于指令遵循、参数知识或文本级一致性）都能被自适应攻击者模仿或优化绕过，亟需更鲁棒的防御方案。

Method: RAGSentinel是一个无需训练、无需标签、适用于黑盒RAG系统的防御方法。它使用代理编码器测量由检索文档诱发的查询条件化隐藏状态偏移，移除共享主题方向，并将中毒文档作为几何异常值从稳健的多数共识中过滤掉。理论上，在诚实多数假设和表示级分离条件下，可精确恢复无毒的多数规模上下文。

Result: 在三个问答数据集、三个LLM家族和多种投毒攻击下，RAGSentinel持续实现低攻击成功率，同时保持竞争性准确率。即使面对具有完整pipeline知识的自适应攻击，防御依然有效。

Conclusion: RAGSentinel通过利用表示层面的几何特性而非依赖LLM的指令遵循能力或文本特征，提供了一种对自适应攻击者更强的RAG投毒防御方案，且无需训练或标签，具有可证明的理论保障。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves the factuality of large language models by grounding responses in external documents, but it also exposes a critical security vulnerability: adversarial documents injected into the knowledge database can enter the context window and steer the model toward targeted incorrect answers. Existing post-retrieval defenses rely on instruction following, parametric knowledge, or text-level consistency, all of which can be imitated or optimized against by adaptive attackers. We propose RAGSentinel, a training-free, label-free defense for black-box RAG systems. RAGSentinel uses a surrogate encoder to measure query-conditioned hidden-state shifts induced by retrieved documents, removes shared topic directions, and filters poisoned documents as geometric outliers from a robust majority consensus. We prove that, under an honest-majority assumption and a representation-level separation condition, RAGSentinel exactly recovers a poison-free majority-sized context. Experiments across three question-answering datasets, three LLM families, and multiple poisoning attacks show that RAGSentinel consistently achieves low attack success rates while preserving competitive accuracy and remaining effective against adaptive attacks with full pipeline knowledge.

</details>


### [192] [WebMCP-Phalanx: Enforcing and Characterizing Trust Boundaries for Browser-Integrated LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24017)
*Lin-Fa Lee,YI-YU Chang,Kuo-Hui Yeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出WebMCP-Phalanx，一种双代理运行时架构，解决WebMCP协议下LLM代理工具调用在多方Web环境中面临的来源伪造、工具生命周期失控和语义提示注入三大风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: W3C WebMCP提案使得LLM代理能够调用网页暴露的工具，但将其集成到基于同源策略的浏览器安全模型中，无法为代理可访问工具提供足够的来源和生命周期保证，带来三种安全风险：主体归属伪造、工具生命周期失控和语义提示注入。

Method: 提出双代理运行时分层的WebMCP-Phalanx架构。第一层提供浏览器原生信任锚，通过加密保护的能力凭证将每个工具绑定到注册主体，并在工具生命周期中传播来源标签。第二层将语义检查与特权工具使用分离：无工具调用权限的隔离代理（Q-LLM）检查工具元数据、输出和页面内容中的提示注入，验证通过的内容再转发给特权代理（P-LLM）执行，同时Q-LLM内部状态对页面脚本隐藏。另提出调用时机门控机制，延迟工具调用直到所有代理可见的工具元数据通过验证。

Result: 浏览器原生所有权机制将撤销与覆盖攻击成功率从100%降至0%；双代理运行时阻断了所有80次嵌入工具描述的提示注入攻击，工具返回攻击仅限80次中的2次成功；任务效用与无攻击基线统计上无显著差异。但白盒自适应攻击者可通过恶意工具名称绕过描述过滤，启发调用时机门控机制。

Conclusion: WebMCP-Phalanx通过双代理分层架构有效缓解WebMCP场景下的三类安全风险，但存在白盒自适应攻击下描述过滤被绕过的局限，需要调用时机门控机制进一步加固。

Abstract: The emerging W3C WebMCP proposal enables LLM agents to invoke tools exposed by web pages. In multi-party web environments, however, integrating agent execution into a browser security model centered on the Same-Origin Policy (SOP) leaves insufficient provenance and lifecycle guarantees for agent-accessible tools, creating three risks: subject-attribution spoofing, uncontrolled tool lifecycles, and semantic prompt injection. We propose WebMCP-Phalanx, a dual-layer agent runtime architecture. Its first layer provides a browser-native trust anchor that binds each tool to its registering principal through cryptographically protected capability credentials and propagates provenance labels throughout the tool lifecycle. Its second layer separates semantic inspection from privileged tool use. A Quarantine Agent (Q-LLM), without tool invocation authority, inspects tool metadata, outputs, and page-supplied content for prompt injection. Validated content is then forwarded to a Privileged Agent (P-LLM) for execution, while the Q-LLM's internal state remains hidden from page scripts. Empirical evaluation shows that the browser-native ownership mechanism reduces revocation and overwrite attack success from 100\% to 0\%. The dual-agent runtime blocks all 80 prompt-injection attempts embedded in tool descriptions and limits tool-return attacks to 2 successful cases out of 80. Across experiments, task utility remains statistically indistinguishable from the no-attack baseline. Under a white-box adaptive attacker, however, description-based filtering can be bypassed through malicious tool names invoked before inspection. This finding motivates a call-timing gate that delays tool invocation until all agent-visible tool metadata has been validated.

</details>


### [193] [What Guides the Agent? Adjudicating Unauthorized Behavior via Localizing Behavior-Guiding Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24022)
*Yichao Gao,Yumo Zhang,Yunhao Yao,Haohua Du,Puhan Luo,Ruiqi Li,Zhiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Attnlocate，一个运行时框架，通过将行为引导指令的定位问题转化为目标检测任务，利用注意力矩阵中的激活痕迹来精准定位影响工具调用决策的上下文片段，从而防御LLM Agent的注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent集成外部资源后容易受到注入攻击，外部不可信数据可能被动态解析为行为引导指令，进而颠覆Agent的决策。现有防御方法仅在输入/输出层面进行静态检测或隔离，无法应对模型推理过程中动态产生的诱导行为。

Method: 将行为引导指令的定位问题建模为目标检测任务：设计多头、多层注意力聚合方案构建面向目标检测的token级特征空间；部署带无锚点检测头的1-D U-Net来检测这些片段；根据检测到的行为引导片段来源提供者的权威性，动态裁决恶意调用尝试。

Result: 在五个LLM家族的十种Agent配置上评估，覆盖间接提示注入和工具投毒场景。平均IoU达0.743，平均AUROC达0.956，在0.067的假阳性率下真阳性率达0.934。且能有效迁移到未见模型，支持无需重训练的权限策略自适应。

Conclusion: Attnlocate通过基于注意力矩阵的细粒度运行时定位方法，有效弥补了现有静态防御的不足，能够精准识别影响工具调用决策的行为引导指令，并在多种模型上展现出良好的泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: LLM agents integrated with external resources gain complex task capabilities, yet the unified natural-language context channel makes them vulnerable to injection attacks: untrusted external data may be dynamically parsed as behavior-guiding instructions during LLM inference, thereby subverting the agent's decision. Existing defenses focus on static detection or isolation of malicious content at the input/output level, remains insufficient for detecting such dynamic inducements that arise during model reasoning. We propose Attnlocate, a runtime framework for fine-grained localization of context spans that genuinely influence tool-calling decisions, i.e., behavior-guiding instructions. Attnlocate casts this localization problem as an object detection task, aiming to detect the distinctive activation traces induced by behavior-guiding instructions within the attention matrix. Specifically, we design a multi-head, multi-layer attention aggregation scheme to construct a token-level feature space tailored for object detection. Then, a 1-D U-Net equipped with an anchor-free detection head is deployed to detect these spans. Finally, based on the authority of the provider from which the detected behavior-guiding spans originate, Attnlocate dynamically adjudicates malicious invocation attempts. We evaluate Attnlocate across ten agent configurations from five LLM families, covering scenarios involving indirect prompt injection and tool poisoning. Attnlocate achieves a mean IoU of 0.743, an average AUROC of 0.956, and a 0.934 true-positive rate at 0.067 false-positive rate. It also transfers effectively across unseen models and supports authority policy adaptation without retraining.

</details>


### [194] [Anatomy of a Scam Call: What 10,000 real scam and spam calls reveal about how phone scammers operate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24127)
*Ethan Traister,Ankit Raj,Jiaqi Gan,Xingyu Shen,Tyler Wu,Yuchen Zhou,Tommy Duong,Kidus Zewde,Siying Chen,Simiao Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了由AI语音代理蜜罐在54天内收集的10,211通诈骗/垃圾电话（913小时音频、330,956轮对话），发现电话诈骗是一个模板化行业：诈骗者保持"办公时间"规律、使用可循环脚本、更多索取身份锚点信息而非支付凭证；随机对照实验表明诈骗者对待不同年龄身份目标的工作强度不同（每十年多15%对话轮次），但索要内容不变；基于开场白的升级预测可达0.87 ROC-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电话诈骗普遍且代价高昂，但其内部运作机制很少在规模化层面被观察到。已有研究多依赖受害者报告或小规模数据，缺乏对完整诈骗呼叫流程的系统性分析，尤其是随机化实验设计和早期检测基准的缺失。

Method: 使用AI语音代理蜜饯收集54天内10,211通入站诈骗/垃圾电话的完整语料；区分"纯诈骗"（索取敏感信息）与"引导营销"（合法但掠夺性的潜在客户生成）；开展随机化实验：每个种子线索携带十个虚构身份之一（均匀随机分配），观察1,823通随机呼叫中目标感知年龄对诈骗行为的影响；另一个实验从诈骗者开场白预测升级（escalation），比较bag-of-words分类器与微调端侧语言模型的性能。

Result: 诈骗呼叫在工作日比周末多6.6倍（"办公时间"模式）；数千个一次性号码运行着少量循环脚本（三十个开场聚类，前五大聚簇占流量一半）；诈骗者更多索取家庭住址和出生日期等身份锚点而非支付凭证。随机实验中，目标感知年龄每增加十年，诈骗者多花约15%对话轮次（比率1.15，95%CI 1.08-1.23，p=0.005），但索要敏感信息的比例不变（OR=0.99/十年）。从开场白预测升级的ROC-AUC从第一句的0.72提升至第八句的0.87，朴素词袋分类器与细调端侧语言模型表现相当。

Conclusion: 电话诈骗是一个模板化行业：它变化的是对目标投入的努力程度（工作强度），而不是索要的内容（目标诉求）。早期检测具备可行性——仅在开场白阶段即可实现高置信度的升级预测，且轻量模型即可达到良好效果。

Abstract: Telephone fraud is pervasive and costly, but its inner workings are rarely observed at scale. We analyze a complete corpus of 10,211 inbound scam and spam calls -- 913 hours of audio and 330,956 transcribed turns from 5,780 distinct numbers -- collected over 54 days by an AI voice-agent honeypot that answered callers and kept them talking, and introduced in a companion data descriptor. We separate outright scams, which solicit sensitive information, from the larger stream of predatory but legal lead generation ("spam") that feeds them. Scam operations keep office hours (6.6x more calls per weekday than weekend day); thousands of disposable numbers run a small catalog of recycled scripts (thirty opening clusters, half the traffic in the top five); and callers solicit identity anchors -- a home address and a date of birth -- far more often than payment credentials, pressing through persistence and manufactured authority rather than overt threats. Our central experiment asks: does it matter who picks up? Every seeded lead carried one of ten fictitious identities drawn uniformly at random, so the identity a fraud operation reaches is fixed before the caller exists. Across 1,823 randomized calls, scammers spent about 15% more conversational turns per decade of the target's apparent age (rate ratio 1.15, 95% CI 1.08-1.23; randomization p = 0.005) -- yet what they asked for did not change (26.3% of calls reached a request for sensitive information; odds ratio 0.99 per decade, 95% CI 0.90-1.08). A second experiment casts early detection as a benchmark: from a scammer's opening lines alone, on a caller-disjoint split, escalation is predictable at 0.72 ROC-AUC from the first line and 0.87 by the eighth, and a plain bag-of-words classifier matches a fine-tuned on-device language model. Telephone fraud emerges as a templated industry that varies how hard it works a target, but not what it wants.

</details>


### [195] [Not All Tokens Are Equal: Region-Aware Consistency Repair of Backdoors in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24354)
*Jiali Wei,Ming Fan,Mingkun Zhang,Haoyu Wang,Jun Sun,Guoheng Sun,Xiaoning Ren,Haijun Wang,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RACER，一个模型级别的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）后门修复框架。它基于"层间不一致异常"的观察，将融合表征分解为视觉和文本token区域，分别归一化层间不一致性并按模态感知权重重组，通过极小极大优化合成最坏情况扰动并进行对抗微调来修复模型，只需100个干净样本即可在36种后门设置下将平均攻击成功率降至1.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型级后门移除方法主要针对传统分类器，在MLLM上效果有限；而MLLM特有的防御多在推理时过滤可疑输入，未能从根本上移除模型内嵌的后门。需要一种能在源头消除MLLM潜在后门的模型级修复方法。

Method: RACER基于关键观察：后门会在内部表征中诱发异常的层到层演化，即"层间不一致异常"，且该异常具有模态依赖性。RACER将融合表征分解为视觉和文本token区域，分别归一化其层间不一致性，并用模态感知权重在深层窗口上重组，形成区域感知的不一致目标函数。通过极小极大优化，合成最坏情况扰动并针对该扰动进行对抗微调，抑制后门行为依赖的深层表征方向偏移。

Result: 在3个开源MLLM上的36种后门设置（涵盖图像、文本和多模态触发器）中，RACER将平均ASR降至1.1%，其中32种设置达到0%，同时保持后门模型和干净模型上的干净任务性能。

Conclusion: RACER有效填补了MLLM模型级后门修复的空白，仅需100个干净样本且无需任何关于触发器、攻击目标或模型是否包含后门的先验知识，即可从源头消除MLLM中的潜在后门。

Abstract: MLLMs are increasingly deployed in user-facing applications, yet they inherit backdoor risks from the pipelines used to construct them: triggers may reside in images, texts, or both. Existing model-level backdoor removal methods, largely designed for conventional classifiers, show limited effectiveness on MLLMs, while MLLM-specific defenses mainly operate at inference time, filtering suspicious inputs without removing the backdoor embedded in the model. To address this gap and eliminate latent backdoors from MLLMs at their source, we present RACER, a model-level repair framework motivated by a key observation: backdoors induce abnormal layer-to-layer evolution in internal representations, which we term the layer-wise inconsistency anomaly. Importantly, this anomaly is modality-dependent, concentrating primarily in the token region encoding the trigger features that the backdoor model actually relies on. RACER therefore decomposes the fused representation into visual and textual token regions, normalizes their layer-wise inconsistency separately, and recomposes them using modality-aware weights over a deep-layer window, yielding a region-aware inconsistency objective that better captures localized backdoor-induced anomalies. Through a min-max optimization, this objective drives worst-case perturbation synthesis and adversarial fine-tuning against the resulting perturbation to repair the model, suppressing the deep representational directional shifts on which backdoor behaviors rely. RACER requires only 100 clean samples and no knowledge of the trigger, attack objective, or even whether the input model contains a backdoor. Evaluations on three open-source MLLMs across 36 backdoor settings spanning image, text, and multimodal triggers show that RACER reduces the average ASR to 1.1%, reaching 0% in 32 settings, while preserving clean-task utility on both backdoor and clean models.

</details>


### [196] [A Drop-in KEM Replacement for Client Signatures in Post-Quantum SSH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24447)
*Hongbo Liu,Yufan Su,Jiangxia Ge,Qionglu Zhang,Zhaoxuan Li,Xianhui Lu,Li Song,Wenhua Gao,Li Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于KEM的SSH用户认证方法，用会话绑定的挑战-响应证明替代客户端公钥签名，在保持公钥凭据模型的同时降低后量子时代的握手延迟和服务器端计算开销。实验表明，该方法在代表性网络条件下与紧凑签名认证相当，且在传输或计算压力大时优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码迁移正在重塑SSH协议，但现有基于签名的认证路径继承了后量子签名的大尺寸和高计算开销，导致延迟、流量和服务器负载增加。KEM认证作为签名中心路径的自然替代方案，在SSH用户认证层（方法可扩展、与传输层隔离、已有通道保护）具有特别的吸引力。

Method: 设计了一种即插即用的KEM用户认证方法，用会话绑定的挑战-响应证明替换客户端公钥签名，适配SSH现有用户认证框架，保留公钥凭据模型并支持增量部署。在OpenSSH中使用liboqs实现，并在后量子ACCE框架下提供基于归约的安理论论证。

Result: KEM认证与紧凑签名认证在代表性网络条件下性能相当；与大型签名混合基准相比，中位握手延迟最多降低约10%。在小TCP初始窗口下，中位延迟较ML-DSA降低7.3%，较SLH-DSA降低17.9%；服务器端在线密码学成本较同NIST类别的ML-DSA降低59.1%。

Conclusion: KEM-based认证是SSH后量子迁移中签名路径的有效替代方案，在保持公钥凭据模型的前提下，能够在后量子签名带来传输或计算压力时提供更优的延迟和更低的服务器端开销。

Abstract: The transition to post-quantum cryptography is reshaping the Secure Shell (SSH) protocol for remote administration. Post-quantum key exchange has been deployed in OpenSSH and is being standardized, while SSH authentication largely remains a signature-replacement effort. This path preserves the familiar public-key credential model, but inherits the size and computation overhead of post-quantum signatures, which can increase latency, traffic, and server-side load. KEM-based authentication offers a natural alternative to this signature-centric path, and SSH makes this especially attractive at the user-authentication layer, which is method-extensible, separated from transport-layer key exchange and host-key authentication, and already protected by the established channel.
  We present a drop-in KEM-based user-authentication method for SSH that replaces client public-key signatures with a session-bound challenge-response proof. The method fits into SSH's existing user-authentication framework, preserving the public-key credential model and enabling incremental deployment alongside existing methods. We provide a reduction-based security argument in the post-quantum ACCE framework, implement the design in OpenSSH using liboqs, and evaluate it under representative RTTs, TCP initial-window settings, and post-quantum migration configurations. Our results show that KEM-based authentication is competitive with compact signature-based authentication under representative network settings, while reducing median handshake latency by up to about 10% against large-signature hybrid baselines. The advantages are clearer when post-quantum signatures stress transmission or computation: median latency under small TCP initial windows falls by up to 7.3% versus ML-DSA and 17.9% versus SLH-DSA, while server-side online cryptographic cost is 59.1% lower than that for ML-DSA in the same NIST category.

</details>


### [197] [Defending Network Intrusion Detection Systems Based on Graph Neural Networks Against Structural Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24454)
*Dimitri Galli,Andrea Venturi,Dario Stabili,Mauro Andreolini,Mirco Marchetti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对抗训练的防御框架，通过策略性地替换良性网络流中的源节点和目的节点来生成对抗样本，从而增强基于GNN的NIDS对结构对抗攻击的鲁棒性。在CTU-13和TON-IoT两个数据集上使用E-GraphSAGE进行评估，结果表明该方法在干净图上具有更优的检测性能，同时提升了对抗结构攻击下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在基于ML的NIDS中表现出色，但对结构对抗攻击（如边注入、节点插入）存在脆弱性，且现有防御方法依赖不切实际的假设，难以适用于真实网络安全场景。因此需要一种更实用且有效的防御机制来保护GNN-based NIDS。

Method: 提出基于对抗训练的防御框架，核心思想是模拟边注入攻击：通过战略性替换良性网络流中的源和目的节点来生成对抗样本，对E-GraphSAGE分类器进行对抗训练，从而在训练阶段引入鲁棒性。

Result: 在CTU-13和TON-IoT两个公开数据集上的实验表明，所提出方法生成的强化检测器在干净图上具有更优的检测性能，并且在面对结构对抗攻击时表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对抗训练是增强GNN-based NIDS防御结构攻击的有效途径，所提出的节点替换策略能高效模拟真实边注入攻击，为实际网络安全场景提供更可靠的防御方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) represent a promising solution for Machine Learning (ML) based Network Intrusion Detection Systems (NIDS), thanks to their ability to leverage both network flow features and topological patterns. While GNN classifiers demonstrate superior robustness against feature-based adversarial attacks compared to other ML detectors, they remain vulnerable to structural adversarial attacks, where an attacker perturbs the underlying network graph topology by injecting edges or inserting nodes. Such attacks pose a realistic and severe threat, undermining the reliability of GNN-based NIDS in practical deployments. While countermeasures have been proposed in the literature, they often rely on assumptions that are unrealistic in real-world cybersecurity scenarios. In this paper, we propose a defense framework based on adversarial training to strengthen GNN-based NIDS against structural attacks. We generate adversarial samples by strategically replacing the source and destination nodes in benign network flows, thereby efficiently mimicking edge injection attacks. We evaluate our approach on two widely used datasets (CTU-13 and TON-IoT) using E-GraphSAGE as the base GNN classifier. Experimental results show that our approach produces hardened detectors with superior detection performance on clean graphs and enhanced robustness against structural adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [198] [Do System Prompts Leave Behavioral Fingerprints? A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Clone Detection via Output Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24461)
*Linghan Chen,Yudong Gao,Jiyao Wang,Kaiyan Ji,Honglong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出黑盒行为指纹（BBF）方法，通过注册模型输出的行为特征来检测商业LLM的提示词是否被克隆部署。大规模实验表明提示词选择解释了24.4%的输出方差，同模型检测AUC达到0.876，跨模型平均AUC为0.725，并能抵抗非自适应提示改写，但面对风格变化时检测性能会显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM的系统提示词可被超过80%成功率提取并无成本重新部署，但提示词所有者无法验证可疑部署是否为克隆，缺乏有效的检测方法。

Method: 提出黑盒行为指纹（BBF）方法：提示词所有者从模型输出注册行为签名，之后测试可疑部署是否与该签名匹配程度高于无关基线。仅需黑盒API访问。另提出诊断查询优化（Diagnostic Query Optimization），一种零成本的查询选择规则。

Result: 在4个模型家族、8个基准、288,000个响应的大规模研究中：提示词选择解释24.4%输出方差；同模型检测AUC 0.876；跨模型AUC范围从0.845（Claude作为检测器）到0.665（Qwen），均值0.725；可抵抗非自适应提示改写（AUC≥0.889）；但对短结构化输出，单句正式语气前缀可使MNLI的AUC从0.978降至0.547；诊断查询优化将跨模型AUC提升+0.120。

Conclusion: BBF是一种有效的黑盒提示词克隆检测方法，但在面对风格变化（style-invariant detection）时检测性能会崩溃，这是需要解决的关键开放问题。诊断查询优化能以零成本提升跨模型检测性能。

Abstract: System prompts can be extracted from commercial LLMs with over 80\% success and redeployed at zero cost, yet a prompt owner has no way to verify whether a suspected deployment is a clone. We propose Black-Box Behavioral Fingerprinting (BBF): the prompt owner registers a behavioral signature from model outputs and later tests whether a suspect deployment matches that signature more closely than an unrelated baseline. BBF requires only black-box API access. Through a large-scale study (4 model families, 8 benchmarks, 288{,}000 responses), we find that prompt choice explains 24.4\% of output variance and same-model detection reaches AUC 0.876. Cross-model performance is bounded by detector identity, with off-diagonal AUC ranging from 0.845 (Claude as detector) down to 0.665 (Qwen) and overall mean 0.725. BBF resists non-adaptive prompt paraphrasing (AUC $\geq 0.889$) and is robust to imperfect extraction, but a single-sentence formal-tone prefix can collapse detection on short structured outputs (MNLI 0.978 $\to$ 0.547), isolating style-invariant detection as the key open problem. Diagnostic Query Optimization, a zero-cost query selection rule, adds $+0.120$ to cross-model AUC.

</details>


### [199] [SeriCrypt: An LLM-Driven Context-Aware Serialization Framework for Cryptographic Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24498)
*Maosong Chen,Xi Chen,Mengcheng Ju,Dongliang Zhao,Chunxiang Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SeriCrypt是一个基于LLM的上下文感知序列化框架，用于自动构建语法正确且密码学有效的协议消息序列。它通过领域特定语言(CDSL)统一表示协议规范中的字段约束、状态依赖和密码学计算规则，并由协议无关的执行引擎自动完成字段值解析、密码学原语调用和字节流序列化。在TLS 1.2/1.3、IKEv1/v2、SSH和TLCP上的评估显示，其生成的消息序列被所有实现接受，发现5个规范违规，并在模糊测试中达到更深的协议状态和更高的代码覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码协议的自动化消息构建长期以来依赖人工操作，现有的自动化方法主要针对文本或明文协议，无法处理密码协议中复杂的跨消息状态依赖和密码学计算约束。

Method: 利用大语言模型从非结构化协议规范中提取字段约束、状态依赖和密码学计算规则，将其转化为统一的CDSL中间表示，再通过协议无关的执行引擎自动完成字段值解析、密码学原语调用和字节流序列化。

Result: 在TLS 1.2/1.3、IKEv1/v2、SSH和TLCP的主流实现上，SeriCrypt生成的消息序列全部被接受并完成握手；安全约束测试发现5个规范违规；同一时间预算下模糊测试达到比主流模糊器更深的协议状态和更高的代码覆盖率。

Conclusion: SeriCrypt展示了LLM驱动的自动化框架在密码协议安全性测试中的实用价值，有效解决了密码协议消息构建的自动化难题，为协议状态机学习、一致性测试和模糊测试提供了可行的支撑工具。

Abstract: Constructing syntactically correct and cryptographically valid message sequences is essential for protocol state machine learning, conformance testing, and fuzzing. Unlike plaintext protocols, cryptographic protocols involve complex cross-message state dependencies and cryptographic computation constraints. Existing automated approaches predominantly target text-based or plaintext protocols, leaving cryptographic message construction largely manual. We present SeriCrypt, an LLM-driven, context-aware serialization framework for cryptographic protocols. It employs a large language model to extract field constraints, state dependencies, and cryptographic computation rules from unstructured protocol specifications into a unified structured intermediate representation, formally characterized by a domain-specific language for cryptographic protocols (CDSL). A protocol-agnostic execution engine parses CDSL declarations, automating field value resolution, cryptographic primitive invocation, and byte-stream serialization. As case studies in protocol security testing, we use the framework to construct violation messages targeting specification-defined security constraints and to support protocol fuzzing, evaluating it on mainstream implementations of TLS 1.2/1.3, IKEv1/v2, SSH, and TLCP. SeriCrypt generated message sequences accepted by all evaluated implementations and completed handshakes in every scenario. Security constraint testing revealed five specification violations, and fuzzing reached deeper protocol states with higher code coverage than mainstream fuzzers under the same time budget, demonstrating the framework's practical value for cryptographic protocol security testing.

</details>


### [200] [Who Falls for SMiSh? Learning Through Survey Data Where to Best Target Awareness Training for Mobile Messaging Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24669)
*Cori Faklaris,Sarah Tabassum,Heather Richter Lipford*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过两项大规模调查，使用模拟方法收集美国成年手机用户的代表性样本数据，发现年轻个体和大学生对SMiShing（短信钓鱼）攻击尤为脆弱，参与者难以正确识别合法信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着手机普及率激增，涉及手机的诈骗（如SMiShing短信钓鱼）日益增多，但缺乏关于谁最容易受到这类攻击的数据，以往针对电子邮件的钓鱼研究提示脆弱性可能因人口统计和情境因素而异。

Method: 采用先前已发表的模拟方法，在美国成年手机用户代表性样本中开展两项大规模调查，收集数据并比较不同人口统计特征和情境因素下的脆弱性，第二项研究还比较了金融信息变体。

Result: 研究发现年轻个体和大学生特别容易受到SMiShing攻击，参与者难以正确识别合法消息，第二项研究提供了金融信息变体的比较结果。

Conclusion: 研究者、监管机构和电信运营商可以通过为24岁以下用户和大学生群体创建手机特定的干预措施，以及增加验证和警告机制，帮助用户防范SMiShing攻击。

Abstract: As mobile phone adoption has surged, so have scams involving these devices. One such scam, known as SMiShing (or smishing) after Short Message Service (SMS), involves fraudsters sending phishing links via mobile texts. Despite the prevalence of SMiShing, there is a lack of data on who is most vulnerable to these attacks. Prior research on phishing (its email counterpart) suggests that susceptibility may vary by demographic and contextual factors. In two large-scale surveys, we use a previously published simulation method to collect data from representative samples of U.S. adult mobile phone users. Our findings indicate that younger individuals and college students are particularly vulnerable. Participants struggled to correctly identify legitimate messages, with the second study providing comparisons of financial message variants. Researchers, regulators, and telecoms can help users by creating mobile-specific interventions for under-24 and university customers and adding verifications and warnings.

</details>


### [201] [ICS Cybersecurity Datasets: A Systematic Meta-Review of Coverage, Evaluation Practice, and Structural Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24757)
*Konstantinos E. Kampourakis,Vyron Kampourakis,Georgios Kambourakis,Sokratis Katsikas,Stefanos Gritzalis,Mario Rodríguez-Béjar,José Luis Hernández-Ramos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对2019至2026年间18项研究中的83个ICS网络安全数据集进行了系统性的元审查，通过统一的五维分类法揭示数据集语料库存在结构性偏差，并指出当前评估实践存在严重不足，最终提出了四项协调的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICS入侵检测研究严重依赖公开数据集，但以往没有工作系统评估这些数据集语料库是否真正支持当前的评估主张，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 对18项研究（2019-2026）进行元审查，从中识别、统一并刻画83个ICS或ICS直接相关的网络安全数据集，采用统一的五维分类法进行分析；同时并行审计评估实践，并进行分类法与评估实践的耦合分析。

Result: 发现语料库存在显著结构偏差：85.5%的数据集集中在后期OT破坏战术，跨阶段IT/OT演进序列仅占8.4%，Purdue层级0级的现场设备证据几乎缺失，运营来源数据仅占15.7%。评估实践方面：零报告采用流式评估，不到一半采用规范的训练/测试划分，仅两项满足可复现性要求。

Conclusion: 识别出三个结构性失衡：架构浅薄性、演进压缩性和跨域替代性，并据此提出了涵盖跨阶段语料库构建、时序结构化基准测试、事件级标签标准和运营数据共享治理框架的协调研究议程。

Abstract: Intrusion detection research in Industrial Control Systems (ICS) heavily depends on public datasets, yet no prior work has systematically assessed whether the collective dataset corpus supports current evaluation claims. This paper addresses this gap through a meta-review of 18 studies between 2019 and 2026, from which 83 ICS, or ICS directly related, cybersecurity datasets are identified, harmonised, and characterised using a unified five-dimensional taxonomy. The taxonomy reveals that the corpus is structurally skewed: 85.5% of datasets concentrate on late-stage OT Disruption tactics, cross-stage IT/OT progression sequences are present in only 8.4% of cases, field-device evidence at Level 0 of the Purdue hierarchy is effectively absent, and operationally sourced data accounts for only 15.7% of the collection. A parallel audit of evaluation practices shows that zero report streaming evaluation, fewer than half apply disciplined train/test partitioning, and only two satisfy reproducibility requirements. Furthermore, a taxonomy-evaluation coupling analysis shows that dataset imbalances constrain the scope and feasibility of several evaluation practices. Based on these findings, we identify three structural imbalances: i) architectural shallowness, ii) progression compression, and iii) cross-domain substitution, and derive a coordinated research agenda which covers cross-stage corpus construction, temporally structured benchmarking, event-level label standards, and governance frameworks for operational data sharing.

</details>


### [202] [Research Methodologies for Cybersecurity in Enterprise Environments: A Narrative Review, Synthesis and Executable Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24850)
*Tran Duc Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对企业网络安全研究面临的方法选择难题，通过对151篇文献的系统梳理，归纳出十一个方法论家族，并将每个家族转化为可执行的协议（含步骤、工具、评估标准、有效性威胁与报告清单）。论文还指出文献中的矛盾（如入侵检测算法排名不一致）应由评估设计差异而非算法本身来解释，最终主张以明确有效性推理为约束的方法论多元主义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业网络安全研究涉及多种方法体系（系统综述、设计科学、受控检测实验、访谈、攻击图模型等），研究者首先面临的是方法选择问题而非技术问题，现有文献缺乏对方法论实践的系统整理与可操作指导。

Method: 对151篇经过验证的文献进行叙述性综述与综合，归纳出十一个方法论家族；为每个家族详细说明其回答的问题类型、证据强度与常见失败模式；将每个家族转化为包含有序步骤、所需工具、评估标准、有效性威胁和报告清单的可执行协议，并以可视化方式呈现。

Result: 建立了覆盖十一个方法论家族的分类体系及对应的可执行协议与可视化映射。通过分析文献中的矛盾（如入侵检测算法排名不一致），论证了这些差异最可能源于评估设计维度的不同，而非算法本身的优劣。

Conclusion: 证据支持以明确有效性推理为约束的方法论多元主义。研究者应使评估设计与所研究决策相匹配，将技术证据与组织证据进行三角验证，明确结果可推广的人群，并报告结果不成立的条件。

Abstract: Enterprise cybersecurity research draws on a wider range of methods than any single community routinely teaches. Researchers face a selection problem before they face a technical one: a study may simultaneously need a systematic review, a design-science artifact, a controlled detection experiment, an interview study, or an attack-graph model. This paper addresses that problem in two ways. First, it provides a narrative review and synthesis of methodological practices across a verified corpus of 151 works. We organise these practices into eleven methodology families, detailing for each what questions it answers, the strength of its supporting evidence, and its common failure modes. Second, we convert each family into an executable protocol comprising ordered steps, required instruments, evaluation criteria, common validity threats, and a reporting checklist. Every protocol is also visually mapped to make the sequence, decisions, and threats legible at a glance. We also treat contradictions in the literature as evidence. For example, reported rankings of intrusion-detection algorithms are wildly inconsistent across individually careful studies. We argue this pattern is most parsimoniously explained by variations in evaluation design rather than the algorithms themselves, as these studies differ in design dimensions known to shift results by more than the margins separating the algorithms. Ultimately, the evidence supports methodological pluralism disciplined by explicit validity reasoning. We conclude that researchers must match their evaluation design to the decision under study, triangulate technical against organisational evidence, explicitly state the population a result generalises to, and report the conditions under which the result would not hold.

</details>


### [203] [Prompt Structure Redistributes, Not Reduces: An Empirical Analysis of Security-Weaknesses in LLM-Generated Python Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24857)
*Maitreyee Das Urmi,Jessica Pourleyli,Fabio Santos,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究基于424个安全敏感的Python编程任务，使用GPT-4o和LLaMA 3.1-8B在五种从结构化到安全导向的提示变体下生成代码，并用Bandit和CodeQL评估。发现结构化提示大幅降低拒绝率，但安全导向的优化并不能持续减少整体漏洞，而是主要改变了风险的分布（高危减少、低危增加）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成代码日益普遍，提示工程被认为是塑造生成软件安全性的关键机制。然而，现有研究多关注漏洞是否存在，缺乏对结构化及安全导向提示如何影响漏洞严重性、类型分布及生成合规性的系统理解。

Method: 选取424个安全敏感的Python任务，在五种逐级叠加结构与安全指导的提示变体下，使用GPT-4o和LLaMA 3.1-8B生成代码，结合Bandit和CodeQL工具从生成合规性、漏洞普遍性、严重性和CWE分布两个维度进行评估。

Result: 结构化提示将GPT-4o的无效输出从338/424降至37-52，大幅提升合规性；但安全导向的提示未持续降低漏洞普遍性。GPT-4o在高危发现上从20.8%降至13.6%，低危发现从32%升至43.5%；LLaMA的变化更弱且不一致。此外还观察到安全驱动的语义漂移现象。

Conclusion: 提示结构能改善生成合规性，但无法可靠替代LLM辅助开发中的稳健安全控制机制。安全提示的作用更多是风险再分配而非风险消除。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly generate code from natural-language prompts, making prompt engineering a key mechanism for shaping the security of generated software. Structured and security-oriented prompts are widely used to encourage safer code, yet their effects extend beyond whether detected weaknesses are simply present or absent. Using 424 security-sensitive Python tasks, we generate solutions with GPT-4o and LLaMA 3.1-8B under five prompt variants that progressively add structural and security guidance, and evaluate them with Bandit and CodeQL along two axes: generation compliance and security weakness prevalence, severity, and CWE distributions. Structured prompting substantially reduces refusals (e.g., GPT-4o invalid outputs drop from 338 of 424 to 37-52), enabling large-scale analysis, but security-oriented refinements do not consistently reduce overall weakness prevalence. For GPT-4o, stronger prompts primarily redistribute risk: high-severity findings fall (20.8% to 13.6%) while low-severity findings rise (32% to 43.5%); LLaMA shows weaker, less consistent shifts. We also observe security-driven semantic drift, where stricter prompts silently remove or rewrite explicitly requested unsafe constructs. Overall, prompt structure improves compliance but is an unreliable substitute for robust security controls in LLM-assisted development.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RENDER基准控制方法，系统性地研究记忆（Memory）和RAG评估中"读者可见的信息呈现形式"对模型回答的影响。通过五级信息包梯子和确定性模板，在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上验证了呈现形式的显著影响：匹配预算的分辨包比最近截断的原始对话高出42.4-72.6分；三种模型在结构化记录上得分为0%，但在自然语言条目上可达45.4-53.4%。研究建议记忆/RAG评估应报告或控制读者可见的呈现形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆和RAG评估往往把回答模型的输入视为实现细节，忽略了同一对话历史可能以记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录等多种形式渲染给模型。这种忽视可能导致评估结果失真，无法真实反映系统性能。

Method: 作者引入RENDER基准控制，包含两个关键组件：(1) 五级信息包梯子（packet ladder），用于定位答案承载内容进入输入的时间点；(2) 确定性模板，近似模拟ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT风格类型化记录和原始对话。在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上进行评估，并辅以HotpotQA迁移测试和检索噪声测试。

Result: 匹配预算的分辨包比最近截断的原始对话高出42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板中，每个模型的最佳-最差差距为24.6-48.8分；在主评分器下，7/9的模型在ChatGPT风格条目上的点估计高于原始对话；三种模型在正式账本式包上得分为0%，但在自然语言条目上达到45.4-53.4%。效果在检索噪声和HotpotQA上均保持。

Conclusion: 记忆/RAG评估中读者可见的呈现形式对模型性能有显著且系统性的影响，评估应报告或控制该变量，否则结果可能产生误导。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [205] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍ESQ-Bench，一个面向企业级Oracle数据库的NL2SQL基准测试，包含三层复杂度体系、六套多数据库schema（465张表、16.4万行数据）及550个金标准问答对。实验显示GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.6在schema链接提示下性能随复杂度层级单调下降，执行匹配率远低于现有基准宣称的89%，且静默分歧严重（EX通过查询中达73-99%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准（如Spider、BIRD）声称执行准确率超过89%，但这些基准基于简化的学术schema和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的真实复杂性，导致模型实际部署效果被严重高估。

Method: 构建ESQ-Bench基准：设计三层企业schema复杂度（465张表、164,682行数据、零空表），在Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL和SQL Server四个数据库上构建相同种子数据的六套schema；开发四指标评估框架（EM精确匹配、EX执行匹配、SR语义正确、SD静默分歧），收集550个金标准验证的问答对（Tier-1:95, Tier-2:228, Tier-3:227），并对多种LLM进行zero-shot和schema链接提示的对比评估。

Result: GPT-4o schema链接提示在三层上的执行匹配率为79.8%、60.3%、57.2%（执行查询），而EM低于7%；静默分歧在EX通过的查询中达73-99%。Claude Sonnet 4.6表现更优（87.4%、74.9%、68.7%），全面超越GPT-4o。GPT-4o zero-shot在三层为78.7%、73.5%、77.8%，在Tier-2/3反超schema链接（因幸存者偏差）。本地Llama 3.2仅达到13.3%的整体EX（73/550），凸显闭源API模型与开源权重模型在企业Oracle schema上的巨大差距。

Conclusion: 现有NL2SQL模型在企业级数据库上的实际性能远低于公开基准报告值，高复杂度schema导致显著性能退化，且静默分歧问题极为严重。即使EX通过的查询中仍有大部分存在语义偏差，这对实际部署构成重大风险。闭源大模型与开源模型在企业场景下的差距巨大，研究社区需要更贴近真实企业环境的基准来推动NL2SQL技术落地。
